L-C, L-L, L-I and L-A apply to every row. A third direction, NEUTRAL, is reserved for a within-band magnitude-only GLIDE and is **not adopted** (new creative direction, OQ-SN-39: default applied 2026-10-27, confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20)) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. The first lie of a world is ALARM by construction (SN-16, The first lie). A kind that is not a row of the table for the subject's true state is not legal for it: FALSE_OK needs a DEGRADED or FAILED subject, FALSE_FAIL a healthy one, and SPRITE_SWAP needs a sheet that carries both frames of the pair. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **L-C, channel.** The target is a module-body-channel surface (a module's sprite, gauge, inspect card or WORN descriptors; LSG LS-14, LSG:864) or, VS-A, a decoy overlay. **Never** any consequence-channel item: trail VFX (mist, drip, frost, scorch, debris, puddle), the `acoustic` classes at a descriptor's anchor, Suit body cues, lamp brightness from `served_pm`, the `AIRFLOW` draught, the `SEAL_STRAIN` strain; nor trails (FT-2), geometry (LY-34, MSA F1), `body.state` or positions (BDY-30), anchors (ART:68-77), the human channel (voice, a crewmate's beam), Attention outputs (AT-17), prompts or durations (IA-10 to IA-13), save or pause surfaces (SL H6). **Never the seam lane either** (MSA F1, MSA-23 fallback): the sprites, glyphs and inspect cards of a welded seam's own PIPE and DUCT valves and its CABLE breaker are not lie targets until the Graph (ASK G8) and Failure & Trail (ASK FT1) publish a consequence-channel lane witness that exists at zero gradient; MSA-35 truth tags (bead, cap plate, socket geometry, `CUT_BLOCKED`, install prompts) are never wrapped. **Never a gouge footprint**: a WORN descriptor whose cause is a real horror's GOUGE (Horror HE-32 footprint, FT:72 caveat) is never a lie target for any seat while that entity is registered. While any seat has a HIDE on an entity, no lie of any class may target a surface the entity has gouged, so the footprint survives on ducts and cables as well as on the hull. **Sanity may not add either**: a false-broken sprite adds no scorch, no sparks-as-arc-POP, no mist, no trail.
- **L-W, witness.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: stated by direction, L-D. "A COMFORT-direction lie" below is a FALSE_OK, a GLIDE toward health or a frame swap to the healthier frame; "an ALARM-direction lie" is a FALSE_FAIL, a GLIDE toward alarm or a frame swap to the worse frame.)* A COMFORT-direction lie (degraded or failed shown healthy) requires a currently active consequence-channel witness for the fault, active in the **fault's compartment** (the compartment holding the node the lying surface reports on), and that compartment must lie within `WITNESS_HOPS_MAX` 2 `[PLACEHOLDER]` compartment hops of the lying surface's compartment over the Graph's `neighbors(cid)` (LS-19); the same compartment is hops 0. An ALARM-direction lie (healthy shown broken) requires that the fault compartment carry no descriptor for the claimed failure AND at least one positive honest cue of health there, drawn from the **closed list** `health_cues` in `lie_targets.json`, authored per module kind (for example `served_pm` lamp brightness and a flowing loop's chevrons; nothing outside the list qualifies), under the same bound. **Absence alone does not qualify**, and "the absence of the claimed trail" is not a member of the list: it was named as a positive cue in the first draft and contradicted this very sentence, so it is removed; a cue is a thing present, never a thing missing. **Perceivability clause** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: a witness at hop 1 or 2 must be reachable by the rung-1 walk at a finite cost (D.7), and where the opening between the compartments is open the consequence classes that cross an opening (the staged fault-1 case relies on HISS and the `AIRFLOW` draught; whether each class is perceivable across an opening is the Graph's and Failure & Trail's to confirm, [UNV], routed in Open Questions) carry to the lying surface's compartment, so the disagreement is observable from where the seat stands; the lie is not created when the rung-1 cost cannot be computed from the layout (AC-SN-134). *(Checkpoint CQ1, closes OQ-SN-15: Player Fantasy target 2 is "walk to the thing the gauge is about"; a same-compartment witness would make every remote gauge un-lieable and empty the lie set on a five-compartment home; a hop count is computable from a published read, a seconds bound is not. L-G and L-S still apply, so a remote FALSE_OK never extends a clock the body is already counting.)*
- **L-2I, a second instrument survives.** For one seat, no loop may have every instrument lying at once. The honest second instrument for a loop is one of: a fixed gauge on the same loop in another compartment; `suit.o2` (the occupant compartment's `p_o2`, Suit VA-16) or `suit.warmth` (that compartment's T and P, Suit VA-19; credited for the COOLANT loop's thermal quantity only, QA-3), each valid **only for the seat's current compartment**; or the scanner (`tool_scanner`, honest in the MVP, SN-19), credited **only if the seat carries it and can equip it** (a seat without the scanner has no honest second instrument on a single-instrument loop, so a lie on that loop is ineligible for that seat, QA-2). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review, BLOCKING: two limits. **(1) Direction.** `suit.o2` and `suit.warmth` are credited only for an ALARM-direction lie (L-D). For a COMFORT-direction lie L-G requires the seat's current compartment band to be OK, so those readings agree with the lie while it is legal and the credit would always be empty; a comfort lie needs a fixed gauge on the loop or the scanner. **(2) Carrying.** "Can equip it" means the seat is not carrying a module: Suit SE-11 answers `equip_tool` with `BUSY` while carrying (read as `carried_instance != NONE`; a seat also needs `tool_held(tool_scanner)`) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G1/G6, creative-director escalation)*, so a seat that carries cannot reach the scanner. A single-instrument-loop lie is therefore not created for a carrying seat, and a PENDING one is INVALIDATED at its activation re-check if the seat is carrying then; once ACTIVE the lie is not re-evaluated against carrying (the same no-uncaused-vanish reason as doors and dropped scanners). The seat drops the module to scan, which is a priced act, not an exploit.)* `suit.integrity`, `suit.reserve` and `suit.charge` measure the suit, not a ship loop, and never count. For POWER, FUEL, COOLANT flow and INTEGRITY only another fixed gauge on the loop or the scanner counts. In the VS-A slice where readings or the scanner may lie, at least one honest instrument must remain per loop per seat (`SECOND_INSTRUMENT_MIN` 1). **L-2I is evaluated at activation.** After activation it INVALIDATES the lie only when a fixed gauge it relied on is destroyed or lost; it is never re-evaluated on seat movement, on a dropped scanner (the scanner stays in the world) or on a door crossing, because a lie that vanished when the seat walked through a door would be an uncaused-vanish tell (QA-2). *(Orchestrator ruling: the rulings' "a gauge on the same loop elsewhere" is unsatisfiable for the slice's single-instrument loops, one fuel tank sight-glass and one reservoir, pump and radiator set; R23's own words "solo MVP needs rung 2 that does not need a walk" are the warrant for counting the scanner and the two loop-quantity Suit readings; checkpoint K2 removed the three Suit keys that measure the suit.)*
- **L-L, leak.** Every lie carries a non-empty `leak_modes` bitmask. HR-5's tri-modal tell (violet gleam, GLIDE motion signature, sound signature) is the default and the **first lie** carries all three; every lie entry's `leak_modes` contains the GLEAM bit (the palette mode) and the motion bit (GLIDE) (bit 2 of the mask, named GLIDE; it stands for the surface class's hue-independent motion mode exactly as Perception Filter PF-15 and `Perceived.leak_modes` define it; Sanity does not define a second set); *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: a needle shorter than the Filter's eligibility floor (`NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` 16, floor 14.3 art px, Perception Filter V.6.3 and E-PF-11) cannot draw the glide, so a lie may claim the motion bit only through a mode its surface can render.)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: conforms to Perception Filter PF-15, BREATH.)* **Motion bit by surface** (adopted from Perception Filter PF-15; the Filter derives the mode from `surface`, nothing is added to `params`): 1 NEEDLE = needle drift plus never-rest wander (the card numeral adds TICK or STALE); 2 READOUT = TICK (GLIDE kind) or STALE plus the gleam breath (FALSE_* kinds); 3 GLYPH and 4 GLOW_SPRITE = BREATH (the false frame is static, the gleam breathes between `floor_eff` and `AMP_MAX_PM` over 4 to 6 s); 5 MACHINE_SPRITE = BREATH for a static-pair frame (housing shown cracked) or the rotor cadence drift for a continuous rotor frame. `lie_targets.json` carries per row `surface`, and for NEEDLE rows `needle_len_px` and the readout modes available (digit mechanism: TICK, STALE); GLYPH, GLOW_SPRITE and MACHINE_SPRITE rows need no extra field. `SANITY_BOOT_LIE_TARGET_DATA` refuses a row only when it can render **no** motion mode: a NEEDLE row with `needle_len_px` below `NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` 16 (floor 14.3) **and** no readout mode, or a `surface` outside 1 to 5. A short-needle NEEDLE row with a readout is legal and its lie takes the readout mode. Rows of surface 3, 4 and 5 are never refused for lacking a needle or readout. Whether the honest alternate frame exists stays the runtime `has_both_frames` gate (SN-16), not a boot lint. The Filter may drop only SOUND, at stages 2 and 3 for plausibility, never below two, so a player with weak audio or weak colour discrimination always retains one (Perception Filter PF-15, PF-G8, C-7). "Subtle is allowed; absent is not" (ART:73). Decoy lies carry the violet rim. A HIDE entry (class a; stage 3 only) carries no per-entity outline, because an outline around empty space would mark the position of a hidden thing: its leak is the standing stage-3 world leak (AD-OQ-2).
- **L-I, immunity.** A subject checked by `field_check` is immune for that seat *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the earlier "or truly repaired or inspected through the full channel" is removed; Interaction IA-30 and its OQ 32 do not adopt a falsification call from repair or inspect completions, SN-17, OQ-SN-12)* for `DEBUNK_IMMUNE_S` 60 s `[PLACEHOLDER]` (safe range 45 to 120, because the build assertion `DEBUNK_IMMUNE_S >= max(LIE_GAP_S)`, 60 >= 45, refuses anything below the longest gap; the window is exclusive of its end run), whether or not it was lying, so uniform immunity leaks nothing.
- **L-A, no alarm class.** A lie never uses alarm-class presentation (the Alarm Red sweep, an alarm sound class); `alarm_eligible` is Graph-owned true state and "alarm class never first" (LSG:563, LSG:1153). A lie never suppresses a true alarm.
- **L-S, severity ceiling for COMFORT-direction lies.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review, BLOCKING: applies to every COMFORT-direction lie (FALSE_OK, a GLIDE toward health, a swap to the healthier frame), L-D; "FALSE_OK" in the rest of this bullet reads "a COMFORT-direction lie" and "hide" reads "show as healthier than".)* Evaluated at activation and on every re-check against the lie's stored `onset_stage`, never the current stage (a stage drop never ends a lie, SN-16); a lie is INVALIDATED only if the subject's true severity worsens beyond what `onset_stage` allows (QA-1). At onset stage 1 a FALSE_OK lie may hide only a subject whose `hp` is DEGRADED (`HP_FAIL` 3000 < hp < `HP_OK` 7000) and whose active forced mode is not in the data list `lethal_fault_modes`; at stage 2 it may hide DEGRADED or FAILED; at stage 3, any module-body fault with an active witness. A subject whose true fault is on the `lethal_fault_modes` list (in the MVP slice the O2 seam leak after a weld and hull fatigue to breach) takes FALSE_OK only at stage `LETHAL_FAULT_MIN_STAGE` 2 or deeper `[PLACEHOLDER]`. *(Orchestrator ruling: adopted from the game-designer's GD-12 and GD-17; the Graph publishes no `lethal_clock_s` read, so the data list is the proxy, OQ-SN-10.)*
- **L-G, loop guard.** No COMFORT-direction lie (any kind, L-D) may be live on a loop while that loop's **guard** (the per-loop table below) reads outside its comfort-eligible condition; the lie is INVALIDATED at the next run. A lie must never extend a lethal clock the Suit's body cues are already counting. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING: the guard was written as a single band read of the seat's compartment for whichever quantity the loop measures, but only `band_o2`, `band_p` and `band_t` exist and no map from loop to band was defined for POWER, FUEL, COOLANT or INTEGRITY, so the Pillar 1/2 rail was undefined for four of five MVP loops; a naive INTEGRITY to `band_p` map would also fire only after a fault-8 breach had started, the very overlap L-G forbids. The table below is the map. `lie_targets.json` carries, per lie-capable surface, its `loop`, and a per-loop `guard` row exactly as below; `SANITY_BOOT_LIE_TARGET_DATA` refuses a comfort-capable surface whose `loop` is not one of the five rows or whose row lacks a guard kind, and an ALARM-only surface needs no guard. A guard whose read is unavailable (no compartment, a hull segment list that does not resolve) is read as not-eligible, never as OK.)*

| Loop | Guard kind | Guard read (all published by the Life-Support Graph, LS-19; Sanity invents none) | Comfort-eligible and kept only while |
|---|---|---|---|
| O2 | BAND | `compartment(cid).band_o2` of the seat's current compartment | `band_o2` is OK (the original rule) |
| COOLANT | BAND | `compartment(cid).band_t` of the seat's current compartment | `band_t` is OK |
| POWER | BAND, downstream | `band_o2` and `band_t` of the seat's current compartment: a bus fault becomes a clock on the seat only through the scrubber and the heater loads | both are OK |
| FUEL | BAND, downstream | `band_o2` and `band_t` of the seat's current compartment: a fuel fault becomes a clock only through a reactor SCRAM and the loads it drops | both are OK |
| INTEGRITY | NODE_READ | `hull_segments_of(cid)` for the seat's current compartment **and** for the lying surface's fault compartment, then `node(id).hp` for each segment, and `band_p` of the seat's compartment | every listed segment reads `hp > BREACH_HP + HULL_GUARD_MARGIN_BP` (BREACH_HP is the Graph's registered 4000 bp, `HULL_GUARD_MARGIN_BP` is 1000 bp `[PLACEHOLDER]`) **and** `band_p` is OK |

*Why the INTEGRITY row reads the plate and not the atmosphere.* The lethal clock of fault 8 (hull fatigue to breach) starts at the breach, but the pressure band moves only after a hole exists; a guard on `band_p` alone would first fire during the clock. The margin is derived from the Graph's own wear: at the worst endogenous multiplier (DERELICT `rate_pm` 2000 times STRESS `stress_pm` 2000, a x4 product) a fatigued plate loses `HULL_FATIGUE_BP_PER_100` 50 x 4 = 200 bp per 10 s period, so 1000 bp is 5 periods = 50 s, longer than the 32 to 38 s the displayed value needs to glide back to the truth (`GLIDE_RETURN_RATE`), so the comfort value has left the screen before the plate reaches `BREACH_HP` at the fastest rate. Fault 8 stays on `lethal_fault_modes` (L-S), so its comfort lies are also stage 2 or deeper. A loop the table does not name is **comfort-ineligible** (ALARM-direction lies are unaffected), which is the fail-safe for the VS-A loops (CO2, fire) until their rows are written. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING; registry request: `HULL_GUARD_MARGIN_BP`.)*
*What L-G bounds, and what the player still sees.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED)* L-G bounds the **ledger**: the lie entry leaves `sanity.perception` in the Sanity run in which the guard fails, so a comfort lie and the Suit's first cue (band `THIN`) are never both *live* for the same seat. The **displayed** needle then glides back to the truth over 32 to 38 s at `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE`, identically for every end type (SNV-11, so the end type is not an oracle); for that window the gauge may still read on the comfort side while the Suit says THIN. This is accepted as diegetic: the needle is visibly leaving the false value while the breath shortens, and the Suit's body cue is the truth channel that never lies. No L-G-specific return rate is introduced (it would make this end type distinguishable from EXPIRED and DEBUNKED, breaking SNV-11). AC-SN-158(c) measures the maximum seconds displayed on the comfort side after the INVALIDATE. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review, BLOCKING: widened from "FALSE_OK" to every comfort-direction kind, and its consequence is now stated rather than left implicit. L-G means a comfort lie's **ledger entry** and the Suit's first cue (band `THIN`) are never both live for the same seat: the entry ends in the same Sanity run in which the guard fails, and the Suit's cue arrives in that same window (the Suit hook runs at divisor 6, offset 2, so the cue may precede the INVALIDATE by 4 or 10 ticks: the Graph commits the band and the Suit cues on a tick T = 6k + 2, and the next Sanity run is T + 10 when T mod 12 = 2 and T + 4 when T mod 12 = 8 *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*); *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED: the earlier wording said the lie and the cue "can never coexist", which holds in the ledger and not on screen, because the displayed needle glides back over 32 to 38 s; see the paragraph above.)* anything that needs a comfort lie and a breath cue together in the ledger is unreachable under this rail, which is why the anchor is staged one hop from the fault. The alternative considered and not adopted was an explicit L-G lead window so that the Suit's first cue is the moment the lie ends while the lie outlives band `OK` by a few seconds: it would let a lie coexist with the first lethal-clock cue, which L-G exists to forbid, and it would need a Pillar 2 review of `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S`.)* It is the sanity-side companion of `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 s.
*Why.* P4 "no lie is unfalsifiable; at least one channel of truth always remains" and P1's closed lists. Each rail answers a named failure: L-C and L-A the consequence channel; L-W and L-2I the cost rule (a lie with nothing to check it against is unfalsifiable); L-L the palette; L-I the sweep exploit; L-S and L-G the cheap death.
*Edge notes.* Proposed but **not adopted**: L-F, a cost floor `VERIFY_FLOOR_S` 1.5 s so that a lie whose witness sits beside its gauge (falsifiable at zero cost) is ineligible (OQ-SN-5). A gauge that carries no remote, magnitude or trend information that a local witness lacks is decoration and a lie on it is a non-event; that is a content gate routed to In-World Instruments and Derelict Authoring (OQ-SN-11), not a Sanity mutator. A surface that is another lie's witness is never itself a lying surface (a witness is a consequence-channel item).

**SN-16. Class (b), false system state: kinds, reach, onset, lifecycle.** *(MVP-live.)*
*Rule.*
- **Kinds** (registry: `lie_kinds`). FALSE_OK (degraded or failed shown healthy); FALSE_FAIL (healthy shown broken); GLIDE (a reading drifts toward the comfort side if the true state is degraded or failed, toward the alarm side if healthy); SPRITE_SWAP (a rotor spinning on a stopped machine, or a housing shown cracked on a whole one). SPRITE_SWAP uses only the machine's own honest state frames, so it is eligible only for a module whose sheet carries both frames of the pair (running and stopped; whole and cracked), a content gate routed to In-World Instruments and Derelict Authoring. The kind is restricted by true state and L-W (SN-15). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review, BLOCKING: the kind is restricted by true state **and by direction**. The **legal-kind set** for an event is the set of kinds with `KIND_W[kind][stage] > 0` whose row in the L-D table fits the subject's true state, whose surface kind can carry the kind (gauge, readout, glyph and card surfaces carry FALSE_OK, FALSE_FAIL and GLIDE; sprite surfaces carry only SPRITE_SWAP), whose direction rails (L-W, L-S, L-G, L-2I) pass for this seat now, whose `has_both_frames` holds for SPRITE_SWAP, which passes the dial-room and zone-edge gates of SN-23 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING; Perception Filter OQ-PF-38 item 3)* and, while `lies_seen` is 0, which is FALSE_FAIL or a GLIDE toward alarm. D.5's weighted pick sums only this set, and its sum `sum_W` is computed **before any draw** (the pre-draw gate below).)* Per-stage weights `KIND_W[kind][stage]` `[PLACEHOLDER]`, comfort kinds (FALSE_OK, GLIDE) weighted above fright kinds (FALSE_FAIL, SPRITE_SWAP) at stages 1 and 2, fright kinds rising at stage 3, SPRITE_SWAP only where the reach allows: stage 1 = 45 / 25 / 30 / 0; stage 2 = 40 / 25 / 30 / 5 (SPRITE_SWAP of a glow sprite only); stage 3 = 25 / 30 / 15 / 30. If the stage-3 machine-frame sets are cut (Instruments IW-34), `has_both_frames` removes SPRITE_SWAP eligibility and the stage-3 weights are renormalised over the remaining legal kinds (D.5's weighted pick sums only legal kinds); *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: for a machine-sprite surface that is the only kind its surface carries, so with no frame pair the legal set is empty and the event is refused without a draw.)* *(Orchestrator ruling: three proposals existed, the creative-director's comfort-versus-fright intent, a flat 40/30/20/10, and per-stage tuples; the per-stage table satisfies the first.)*
- **Reach by stage (the intensity ladder).** Stage 1: gauge needle, readout (the card numeral, the `displayed_value` of the needle ref's `Perceived`; TICK and STALE are modes of that numeral only, Instruments C2) and the inspect card (the card is the same instrument as its wall gauge and lies exactly when the gauge lies, ART 7.0 and HR-14; a card that stayed honest while its gauge lied would make inspecting a free falsifier, the failure KRN:96's design test names; AD-C1). Stage 2: plus state glyph and the lamp-housing glow sprite (the only route by which a "light" lie exists in the MVP; the real lamp brightness stays true). Stage 3: plus the whole machine sprite ("the lie extends from the gauge to the sprite"). Intensity scales by count (`LIES_MAX[stage]` 1 / 2 / 4), kind and reach, **never** by removing a leak or touching a witness.
- **Lit-entry event (the only onset trigger).** The lookup chain uses real queries: `PlayerId` -> actor (the kernel binding table) -> Body `actor_section(actor_id)` and `actor_local_px(actor_id)` (BDY-29) -> Layout `room_at(section_id, px)` (LY:296) -> `compartment_of_room(room_id)` (LY:297), or Graph `compartment_at(room_ref)` (LS-19) -> Graph `nodes_in(cid)` -> Emitter `lit(ref)` (EM-25). Every query exists on disk; the mapping from a Graph node id to the OBJECT subject ref that `lit(ref)` answers for is now answered *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: it is authored data. In-World Instruments IW-31 and Derelict Authoring DA-34 author an instrument placement record `{instrument_id, node_ref, mount, host_face}`, Interaction registers each as an `INSTRUMENT` OBJECT subject through `sync_section` from Layout's `subject_anchors` (IA-37), and the Derelict Authoring lint `INSTRUMENT_NO_BINDING` is an ERROR. Without that binding the MVP would raise zero lies, so the slice-level check is AC-SN-134 and the new AC-SN-161; OQ-SN-33 is closed as answered)*. For each seat P the **candidate set** is `C(P) = nodes_in(cid(P)) ∩ {lit}`, restricted to lie-eligible module-body subjects, and the visit set `evented[]` records the subjects that have had their one event. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the rule is rewritten. The first draft rolled whatever the chest lamp's doorway sweep hit first, so eligibility was biased toward gauges near the door and could be steered by the player's facing, and a thrown glowstick could pick which subject is rolled.)* An **event** for P is the **first illumination of a subject S during a visit**, by any source: S is in `C(P)` now AND S is not in `evented[]`. A **visit** is the span from P's compartment changing to P's compartment changing again, detected as `cid(P) != last_cid` where `last_cid` is the persisted ledger field written at step 7 of every executed run (so a save between runs and a restore see the same previous compartment) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING)* (`entry_pending` is set on the change and clears on the first run at which `C(P)` is non-empty, which opens the batch window below); `evented[]` is emptied when the compartment changes. S is added to `evented[]` when its event is formed, whatever the gates later decide, so **one event per (seat, subject, visit)**: turning, facing, toggling the lamp or a fixture relighting an already-evented subject inside the same compartment is not an event, and the cause of the illumination is not consulted (the first draft's (a) entry batch and (b) non-own-beam cause, and with them the need for `lit_static`, are retired; `lit_static` is published by Emitter State, EM-25, and is no longer required by this rule). **Batch window:** the events formed in the first `ENTRY_BATCH_RUNS` 10 `[PLACEHOLDER]` runs (2 s) from the run that opens a visit's batch (`batch_end_run`) are held and processed together at the window's close in the order below, so a doorway sweep that lights one gauge a moment before its neighbour does not give the first one the cap; an event formed after the window closes is processed in the run it forms. **Compartment change while events are held.** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G4)* The compartment-change check (`cid(P) != last_cid`) is the first act of step 5, before event formation and before the batch close. While `batch_end_run` is set, nothing has been processed, so the held events are exactly the `evented[]` entries (no new ledger field). If the check fires on any run up to and including `batch_end_run`, the held events are discarded: `evented[]` is emptied (as on every change), no debounce stamp is written, no draw is made, no counter advances, `visit[p][u]` is unchanged, `batch_end_run` is cleared to its no-batch value, and `entry_pending` is set for the new compartment. A change on `batch_end_run` + 1 or later finds the events already processed at `batch_end_run`. A discarded subject can form a fresh event on a later visit with no debounce history from the discarded one (EC-SN-64). **Scope:** `C(P)` is read for the seat's own compartment only, so a glowstick or fixture lighting a subject in another compartment produces no event for P, and a glowstick that lights a subject inside P's compartment lights it for everyone alike (a lit-only trade, the player choosing what to light, not a lever on the dice: the subject's roll is the same whoever or whatever lit it). Each subject has a per-seat debounce `LIT_ENTRY_DEBOUNCE_S` 20 s `[PLACEHOLDER]` (a no-draw gate, both runs being PROCESSING runs (the batch close, or the formation run when the window is closed): an event processed fewer than 100 runs after the processing run P at which that subject's last event passed the debounce (the stamp) is dropped without a draw and does not refresh the stamp; one processed at P + 99 is refused and one processed at P + 100 passes; formation-to-formation spacing is irrelevant because the stamp is written at processing time *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G4)*; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the stamp is written when an event passes the debounce gate, whatever a later gate decides, so the stamp and the draw counter stay independent and replay-stable)*; with one event per visit the debounce now bounds leave-and-re-enter spam, which opens a new visit each time). A compartment where nothing is lit has an empty `C(P)` and produces **zero** onsets. *(Orchestrator ruling: this defines R16's term; the chest lamp is per-facing and sweeps subjects in and out of the beam continuously, so a bare edge detector would spam the draw. `entry_pending` persists until the seat leaves because a timed variant lets a player stay unlit for a few seconds, then light up, and receive no lies, a free dodge.)*
- **Pre-draw gates.** Before any draw: class (b) enabled; stage at least 1; active plus PENDING lies for the seat below `LIES_MAX[stage]`; at least `LIE_GAP_S[stage]` (45 / 25 / 10 s) since the seat's last onset (`run - last_onset_run >= 5 x LIE_GAP_S[stage]`, inclusive, or `last_onset_run` is -1; `last_onset_run` is the persisted ledger field set to `run` on the **accept** run by D.5, not on PENDING to ACTIVE, which AC-SN-150 and AC-SN-173 pin) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING)*; the subject passes SN-15; the subject is not immune; **the legal-kind set is non-empty (`sum_W >= 1`)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review, BLOCKING: the set is computed from the true state, the L-D direction rails, the surface kind, stage reach, `has_both_frames`, and the first-lie restriction below, and `sum_W` is its weight total, before any draw. When `sum_W` is 0 no draw is made and no counter advances. Without this gate a FAILED subject at stage 1, a DEGRADED lethal-mode subject at stage 1 or a machine-sprite surface with no frame pair would have consumed the accept draw and then called `rng_int(1, 0)`, which breaks the fixed 1-or-4-draws replay guarantee. `sum_W >= 1` is a precondition of D.5's second `rng_int` call.)*; the subject has no live (PENDING or ACTIVE) lie for this seat (**at most one live lie per (seat, `subject_ref`)**; the inspect card is a surface of that same lie, never a second one; so the Filter never has a collision to resolve; Perception Filter OQ-PF-16). An event that fails a gate performs **no** draw and advances no counter (the lie stream is `"sanity.lie/<PlayerId>/<stable_id>"`, one counter per subject counting only that seat's gated events on that subject, kept in a map in `sanity.ledger` and pruned on subject destruction), so the cap cannot be used to burn draws and an unrelated lit-entry elsewhere cannot shift this subject's dice. Events in one run are processed in the order of `rng_derive(world_seed, "sanity.lie.order/<p>/<stable_id>", the subject's visit counter)` ascending, ties broken by ascending `stable_id` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: a hash can collide, and an unbroken tie would leave the order to Dictionary iteration, which is [UNV])*, a pure function that advances no counter, so low subject ids have no standing preference. **Gate evaluation order** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED: the debounce writes a persisted stamp, so its position relative to the pre-draw gates must be one order, or two faithful implementations hash differently)*: events are formed at step 5 of the run (adding the subject to `evented[]`), held until `batch_end_run` if inside the batch window, and **then processed one at a time in the order above, each through these gates in this fixed order (which supersedes the order in which the gates are listed above), stopping at the first refusal**: (1) the per-subject debounce, evaluated at **processing time** (batch close, or the formation run if the window is closed), which writes its stamp the moment the event passes it; (2) class (b) enabled; (3) stage at least 1; (4) the cap `LIES_MAX[stage]` (counting PENDING plus ACTIVE lies, including those accepted earlier in this batch); (5) the gap (`last_onset_run`, updated by an accept earlier in this batch, so a second event in one batch is gap-gated); (6) the subject passes SN-15; (7) not immune; (8) no live lie on the subject; (9) the legal-kind set is non-empty (`sum_W >= 1`); then the draws of D.5. A refusal at gates (2) to (9) leaves the debounce stamp as written and advances no draw counter.
- **Onset.** D.5: a gated event accepts with probability `ONSET_PM[stage]` per mille (150 / 300 / 550 `[PLACEHOLDER]`), with no repair weighting at the MVP default `REPAIR_BIAS_W` 1 (user ruling OQ-SN-40: the 2x "it came undone" weight on a subject repaired within `REPAIR_BIAS_S` 60 s is dropped from the MVP; it stays a knob, `REPAIR_BIAS_W` above 1, with the `repaired_recent[]` field kept for it) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling G2: the field is reserved and always empty at the MVP, SN-17)*; on accept it draws kind, life (`LIE_LIFE_S` 40 to 120 s via `rng_int`) and activation delay (`ONSET_DELAY_RUNS` 10 to 25), and stores `onset_stage`, the stage at the accept. **Lie timing:** `start_run` is the accept run; `active_run = start_run + delay_runs` (published in the perception entry; Perception Filter OQ-PF-17); `end_run = active_run + life_runs`, so the life is counted **from activation** and is the whole ACTIVE span (the Filter's D.2 `L_s` holds as written). `first_lie` is true on the entry accepted while `lies_seen` is 0 and is stored with it, so the Filter knows which entry keeps all three leak modes. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: `lies_seen` is incremented when an entry steps from PENDING to ACTIVE, not on accept, so a first lie invalidated while PENDING does not consume the teaching slot; the accept also stores `direction` (L-D).)*
- **Lifecycle.** PENDING (accepted, invisible for the delay so the activation can re-validate SN-15) to ACTIVE (in `sanity.perception`) to one of EXPIRED (`end_run` reached: the time rung), DEBUNKED (`record_falsification` only, SN-17; a PENDING lie can be DEBUNKED too), INVALIDATED (a guard rail no longer holds, the subject is destroyed or its `subject_ref` no longer resolves, the true state now equals the displayed state or the derived `direction` no longer equals the stored one). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED: the reveal-by-full-channel cause is deleted from DEBUNKED, since a repair or inspect completion calls nothing (SN-17); the class-switched-off cause is deleted from INVALIDATED, since SN-28 says a class switched off lets live lies **expire normally** with no new onsets (no uncaused vanish); and PENDING to DEBUNKED is added to the transition table.)* All three end types leave `sanity.perception` on the same run and none is announced: the Filter returns the displayed value to the truth on the glide path at its `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` (registry; never a snap, never a settle) with the leak amplitude following the remaining displayed-minus-true discrepancy, identically for EXPIRED, INVALIDATED and DEBUNKED, so the end type is never an oracle (ART 7.3, SNV-11, AD-C2). The lie's clock runs in sim time: if the seat leaves the compartment the clock keeps running, the entry stays listed with `away` true, and the Filter draws the surface wherever it is visible (through an open door, at far zoom); crossing a hatch is not a lifecycle event and starts no return *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G6 GAP, supersedes the 2026-10-27 delegated G1 listing rule)*; a witness that ends before `end_run` (the fault was repaired) collapses the lie at the next run. **A stage change never ends a lie**: existing lies run to expiry and only new onsets use the new stage's caps and reach (a lie that vanishes exactly at a stage boundary is a tell of the boundary and uncaused pixel motion). *(Orchestrator ruling: SD proposed invalidating on a stage drop, GD keeping; keeping is adopted.)*
- **The first lie.** `lies_seen` (ledger, WORLD) starts at 0. The first lie ever raised in a world is FALSE_FAIL or GLIDE, on a subject whose loop has no fault and no forced mode, with all three leak modes. It costs the player one `field_check` (`FIELD_CHECK_TICKS` 120 = 2 s) plus `T_EQUIP` plus reach (D.7) and teaches the ladder before a costly lie can land. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the first lie is **ALARM-direction by construction** (a healthy gauge shown worse), so it is cost-free to ignore and teaches the gleam, the glide and the scanner, not the anchor's lesson; the anchor is the first costly lie, lie 2 or later. Acting on it has a **bounded cost**: a repair channel on a hallucinated-broken healthy subject runs its full, truth-independent duration with TOOL noise and delivers its true reason as the ordinary outcome (Interaction IA-12, IA-13), one wasted channel and no more, and it does not debunk the lie. While `lies_seen` is 0 a first-lie event is formed only when the seat has no live lie, so at most one first-lie candidate exists, and the legal-kind set is restricted to the two ALARM kinds on a fault-free subject, which folds the first-lie rule into the pre-draw gate: a first lie that cannot be raised is deferred with no draw. On the home ship during the post-sortie hangover (SN-29) the first lie is the likeliest one to land; that is intended, it is harmless.)* *(Orchestrator ruling: adopted from GD-14 on competence grounds; if no fault-free lit subject exists the first-lie onset is deferred, nothing is raised and `lies_seen` does not advance until one does, because a costly first lie would defeat the purpose, QA-13.)*
*Why.* Lies meet the player where they look. The lit-entry rule keeps every lie inside the seat's light (a compartment where nothing is lit yields no lies, and a seat that keeps its own lamp off there gives up the lit-only verbs and the readings: a trade, not an exploit) and keeps the draw counter a function of the player's own actions (SN-24).
*Edge notes.* All lies are per seat: two seats at different stages see their own view of one gauge, which is what makes rung 3 work. A subject eligible for both FALSE_OK and FALSE_FAIL takes its kind from its true state. If a lit-entry batch holds more candidates than the cap allows, draws stop at the cap and the remainder advance nothing. -> ADR candidate: the candidate-set data format, a Sanity-owned `assets/data/sanity/lie_targets.json` mapping module kind to surface kinds to witness descriptor modes and the `lethal_fault_modes` list; no lie is encoded in any authored template (DA-37).

**SN-17. The falsification sink, the room rung and the reveal price.**
*Rule.* **`record_falsification(player_id, check_kind, subject_ref, outcome)`** (the signature ratified verbatim from IA-30) is a **Class B** mutator called by Interaction's `field_check` **hook on the channel's completion tick only**, never from `apply` (which runs at the begin tick and would debunk a lie at the press), never at begin, resume, abort, cancel or restore (Interaction IA-30 and IA-14 row 9, AC-IA-55) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the first draft said "apply")*. `outcome` is the channel result Interaction knows (COMPLETED); **Sanity derives whether a lie existed from its own ledger and never from the argument** (C5, a wording note to Interaction). Effect: any ACTIVE or PENDING lie on `subject_ref` for `player_id` becomes DEBUNKED (`debunked_run` stamped), L-I immunity starts for `DEBUNK_IMMUNE_S`, and the **level is unchanged** (no grounding gain, and no penalty for a check on a non-lie). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the first draft added "the same effect applies when a repair or inspect channel completes", requested of Interaction. Interaction does not adopt that call (IA-30, its OQ 32: a repair completion does not debunk). The fallback that the first draft held in reserve is therefore the rule: a repair or inspect channel on a hallucinated-broken subject delivers its true reason only as the ordinary outcome after the full channel (IA-10 to IA-13), never instantly, and **does not call this mutator**, so it ends nothing and stamps no immunity; the lie runs to expiry or is INVALIDATED by the rails. The room rung's look (a walk, a listen) likewise calls nothing. Only a `field_check` debunks. A repair of a truly degraded subject is also seen as a Graph `hp` rise (a rise of any amount at a repair completion or from any Graph repair mutator), which ends a comfort lie by the witness ending (L-W); OQ-SN-12 is closed as declined.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling G2 (the creative-director's resolution of the `repaired_recent[]` disagreement): `repaired_recent[]` is a **reserved, always-empty ledger field with no MVP writes**. Stamping it from an `hp` rise would need a per-subject last-seen-`hp` map in the ledger, state the MVP does not otherwise need now that `REPAIR_BIAS_W` is 1 (OQ-SN-40); the field stays in the SHAPE key set (no format change when the knob is enabled), and when `REPAIR_BIAS_W` above 1 is enabled post-MVP the stamp is written from a Graph repair event that the Graph is asked to publish (cross-GDD note, OQ-SN-46), never inferred by Sanity from `hp` deltas.)*
*Off-run call index and the lag.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED)* The completion tick is usually not a multiple of 12, and the mutators run in `interaction.step` outside a Sanity run. A mutator call at kernel tick `T` stamps `run_call = (T + 11) div 12`, the index of the **next** hook run to execute (on an aligned tick `T mod 12 = 0` it is `T div 12`, because `interaction.step` precedes `sanity.drift`, so the check lands first; otherwise it is `T div 12 + 1`). `debunked_run` is `run_call`, the immunity `until_run = run_call + 5 x DEBUNK_IMMUNE_S`, and the ledger mutation (state DEBUNKED, `immune[]` entry) is applied **immediately** on the call so `state_hash` agrees at the next tick, while `sanity.perception` is rebuilt at the next run as always. Consequence, accepted and stated: after a scan completes at `T mod 12 = r` the needle keeps lying for up to 11 ticks (0.18 s) until that run, since the Filter reads the rebuilt projection; the scan's two-second bar plus the 11-tick lag are both inside the glide-return window the player already sees (SN-16), and the lag is bounded by the divisor, so it needs no cap knob. AC-SN-66 and AC-SN-128 carry an unaligned case (`T mod 12 = 5`).
*Reveal price (KRN:96, KRN:202).* "Sanity prices how a reason reaches a hallucinating player": a reason code is computed from true state and the kernel never filters it; the price is paid in channel time, light and presence. Durations are identical for hallucinated-broken, truly broken and destroyed subjects (IA-12, IA-13), no PLAYER `validate` reads module-body `hp`, `state`, `forced_mode` or `served_pm` (IA P4 lint), and a rejection must not let the player falsify a hallucination at zero time, risk or trust cost. Kernel OQ 14 (commands aimed at phantoms) is CLOSED by IA-13 and is not reopened: phantoms have no subject, are never `lit`, never take focus, and nothing is submitted or rejected.
*Why.* Sanity stays a pure function of exposure and recovery (no meta-farming, no "reward path" smell); the ladder's payoff is certainty (the P4 cost rule). The scanner result is never stored as truth about a lie in `interaction.state` (IA-30).
*Edge notes.* A `field_check` on a subject with no lie stamps immunity, changes nothing visible, and leaves the level unchanged. If a check completes on the same hook tick as the lie's `end_run`, the check lands first (`interaction.step` precedes `sanity.drift`, SN-2): the lie is DEBUNKED with immunity, which the player cannot tell from EXPIRED followed by immunity on a non-lying subject. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* A debunked lie stops lying through the same glide-return as any other end, but the immunity window is uniform, so the player cannot infer from its presence whether a subject lied.

**SN-18. The verification ladder.**
*Rule.* Rungs, in the Graph's order (LSG:44): (1) **the room itself**; (2) **a second instrument elsewhere on the ship**; (3) **a saner crewmate** (VS-B); (4) **time**. Every lie has a stated finite cost on at least two rungs, guaranteed by L-W (rung 1) and L-2I (rung 2). The time rung is the lie's remaining life, at most `LIE_LIFE_S` max (120 s `[PLACEHOLDER]`), and its price is the real fault bleeding the room meanwhile, so the world cost of waiting out the maximum life must be non-trivial for every MVP fault (an AC in Acceptance Criteria). Cost columns carried by formula D.7: time (s), lit required, noise class, tool swap, reach, trust. A saner crewmate costs trust: believing a friend's word over one's own eyes. A crewmate is drawn as a horror only to an actor whose `harm_eligible` bit is set (SN-20), and the human channel (voice, beam) is never altered, so a saner crewmate's voice is always a true instrument. The `field_check` is the shortcut that turns a rung-1 or rung-2 observation into a cleared ledger entry at `FIELD_CHECK_TICKS` cost. The break-even between walking to the witness and scanning from where one stands is `d* = (FIELD_CHECK_TICKS / TICK_RATE) x (V_WALK x TICK_RATE / TILE_PX)` = 2 s x (2 x 60 / 48) = 2 s x 2.5 tiles/s = 5 tiles; a witness nearer than 5 tiles is cheaper to look at, farther is cheaper to scan.
*Why.* "Checking costs time, risk or trust" (P4): each rung prices a different currency, so no single habit (scan everything, trust the gauge, wait) dominates. The immunity window is uniform and short enough that a sweep of every surface on entry is not dominant at two or fewer eligible surfaces (`DEBUNK_IMMUNE_S` 60, safe range 45 to 120; OQ-SN-7 closed).
*Edge notes.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: status refresh.)* The equip swap to scanner slot 3 **is registered**: Suit SE-11 sets a sim swap duration of exactly 0 ticks (the Controller's hand animation is presentation only), so `T_EQUIP` is 0 by rule and OQ-SN-6 is closed; scan costs in D.7 remain **lower bounds** for the other reasons (the look, a doorway crossing, the Interaction press latency, and reach), and `equip_tool` is `BUSY` while carrying (L-2I). `field_check` **has** a declared noise class: TOOL, pushed per accrued tick (IA-30, IA-33), so a stationary scan is never free of Attention and the half of OQ-SN-13 that asked for a class is closed (the exact AU weight stays Attention's and Interaction's). **The intended role of the tell** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: every lie carries a gleam and a motion bit (L-L), so after the first lie **detection is free** and a lying surface announces itself to a player who is looking at it; "subtle is allowed, absent is not" (ART:73) is accepted as the price of fairness. What the ladder prices is **confirmation and direction**: the player learns *that* a gauge lies for nothing and pays to learn *what is true* (a walk and a listen, a scan with its two seconds and its TOOL noise, or a wait). The scanner is therefore not a free instrument: it carries a second currency, TOOL noise (Pillar 3), beside its two seconds of lit channel; a further scanner cooldown or a within-band magnitude-only GLIDE (so the direction cannot always be inferred from the inversion) were proposed and are **not adopted** here, being new creative direction (OQ-SN-39: default applied 2026-10-27, confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20), none of the three at the MVP) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. Targeted scanning (scan the surfaces that glide, not the room) is the expected habit and D.12 states the cost model for it; rung-mix telemetry (AC-SN-165) tells a playtest whether one rung dominates.

**SN-19. The scanner, the Suit readings switch and the rest of the instruments.**
*Rule.* In the MVP the scanner (`tool_scanner`, slot 3, `charges_max` 0, SE-11) is an **anchor**: its reading is honest, unwrapped, a reading in the reading register, never a verdict icon, never a real-or-fake binary, never Alarm Red or safe green (IA:972, F-3). In VS-A it may be lie-capable at stage 3 only, behind `SCANNER_LIE_STAGE` (default never), with the HR-5 tri-modal tell. Its price is `FIELD_CHECK_TICKS` 120 (Interaction's, cited and never re-valued) of lit channel plus the equip to slot 3 plus reach `REACH_PX` 72 plus Interaction's declared noise class. Sanity states a floor rationale `FIELD_CHECK_FLOOR_TICKS` 90 (a multiple of `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6) below which a check is nearly free, routed to Interaction's knob note (REG:4919). All five Suit `true_value` keys (`suit.o2`, `suit.reserve`, `suit.integrity`, `suit.warmth`, `suit.charge`) are **honest in the MVP** (SE-29, locked). The VS-A per-key switch `READING_LIE_STAGE[key]` defaults to never; the designed order is `suit.warmth` first (the teaching beat: the numeral says warm, the breath says cold), then `suit.o2` and `suit.charge`, with `suit.integrity` and `suit.reserve` last. Body cues (breath, visor fog, rime, shiver, reserve valve click, load layer, seam jet, HEAVY back-frame, stagger, walk-only) are never corruptible (SE-25, SE-27). Rail counts (the glowstick; the tool suppressant is removed from the MVP start kit by user ruling X6, 2026-10-27, so it has no rail count to lie about) stay anchors (SE OQ-6) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; the ship-alarm and world-housing warning marks never lie (Graph `alarm_eligible` only); a suit-rung mark sits beside its reading and inherits that reading's class (HUD HS-30; the creative-director's recommendation, user confirmation pending, OQ-SN-36; at the MVP no reading lies, so every mark is honest). The flip of a key from honest to wrapped is the Filter's, on Sanity's switch. Suit never reads sanity; no Suit cue changes with sanity.
*Why.* Pillar 2 protects a first-time player's only death clock until the ladder is playtested (SE:103); the solo MVP needs a rung 2 that does not need a walk (R23).
*Edge notes.* The carried risk (SE:103) is that if VS-A never turns the switch on, the warmth teaching beat never ships; it is recorded as an Open Question (SE OQ-15). The "closed list" of Suit body cues quoted by the Graph (LSG:168, LSG:209, LSG:864) still lists "vignette"; Suit dropped it (SE-27, SE:132), so this GDD quotes Suit's current set (C7, a refresh owed to the Graph owner).

#### The other three classes (designed to the same depth; deferred, behind `CLASSES_ENABLED`)

**SN-20. Class (c), crewmates as horrors.** *(Deferred: blocked on Co-op, VS-B; exercised now with stub actors.)*
*Rule.* While actor A's `harm_eligible` bit is set, every other bound seat is drawn as a horror **for A only**, continuously, at the crewmate's **true position**, with the rig driven by the crewmate's **true velocity**, with the crewmate's **true beam** (shape, colour and chest-point origin) and **true voice** (ART 5.5 names beam shape, fringe and voice as three redundant tells, any one of which suffices; the beam and the voice are the human channel and are never altered). Victim side: A's `tell_on` drives the victim's tell (a violet fringe 2 px outside the contour and along both beam edges, breathing every 4 to 6 s, drawn in an unlit pass, casting no light, raising no Attention and silent; ART 5.5, HR-7), plus the honest P1 proximity cue from A's suit (physically real, played on every client; whether the source hears it is Adaptive Audio's). A hallucination alone harms nobody: harm still needs a deliberate lit `HARM_CHANNEL_TICKS` 204 channel with a cutter or welder (IA-31). The act is lethal and attributed to the web (SN-12, SN-13). Monsterisation never touches `body.state`, positions, prompts or durations. Ladder (D.7 row): rung 1 hear the voice and see the true beam colour; rung 2 a second look from another position *(a crewmate has no instrument, OQ-SN-8)*; rung 3 ask a saner crewmate; rung 4 leave eligibility (level above `SAN_HARM_EXIT`). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)*
*Why.* It closes IA-31 precondition (1). Lead time from the victim's tell to eligibility is at least 83.4 s plus the 30 s dwell, 113.4 s, at the worst-case cap (SN-10).
*Edge notes.* **Pre-eligibility glimpses are rejected**: a crewmate flickering as a horror before the bit sets would reproduce the IA-31 leak (a horror shape without a plate means it is a crewmate); the preface to class (c) is carried by classes (a) and (b) and by the victim tell. An eligible actor stays eligible until `level > SAN_HARM_EXIT`: from the worst-case overshoot during the dwell (about 15000 - 150 x 36 = 9600) recovery to 20000 takes 10400 / 12 = 867 runs = 173 s in a settled home compartment, but 34.7 minutes at `REC_IDLE`; the group's practical cure is to bring the seat home. That is the intended co-op beat (escort) and belongs in Game Feel. Counterplay to the act itself is movement and voice: a victim who leaves reach within the channel survives (Interaction's `harm_step` accrues by reach).

**SN-21. Class (d), phantom salvage.** *(Deferred: VS-A, class (d); MVP ships none. Boarding & Salvage is designed.)*
*Rule.* Junk defs are real world scrap (IDB-22, ids 9000 to 9999, VS only), never per-player objects. Sanity dresses them per seat from a **Sanity-owned** lure-band file, `assets/data/sanity/lure_bands.json`, `{band_id, mid_g, class}`; the choice is deterministic from (instance id, seat, `sanity.lure` counter) via D.6, stored in `lures[]` (at most `LURES_MAX[stage]` = 0 / 2 / 3 per seat `[PLACEHOLDER]`, and never more than `LURE_FRACTION_MAX_PM` 400 per mille of the junk in the seat's lit set). A lure is eligible only if its pretended `weight_class` differs from the junk's true class AND the mass gap is at least `WEIGHT_TELL_MIN_PM` 300 (cited); otherwise the breath-step falsifier cannot work. **Real valuables are never dressed as junk** (anti-P5). A seat whose `cue_load` is HEAVY through stowed items gets no lure, because stowed HEAVY masks every carried class ("drop the plate first", SE OQ-8). More generally (Suit SE-28), `cue_load = max(carried, stowed)` with `STOWED_CUE_FLOOR_CLASS` MEDIUM, so the carried-weight field check is valid only while the stowed class is below the carried class under test. Cheap checks, cheapest first: (1) the **weight breath-step** (Interaction's VS-A `check_kind` HEFT, IA-30: pick it up; the Suit's load cue differs from the lure's class); (2) **SCAN** (`FIELD_CHECK_TICKS`); (3) **PALETTE** (VS-A: the violet leak on the sprite); (4) the **authored-absence read** (undisturbed dust, a missing verdict trail, DA:1176; a module's own fitting may carry a dried trail, a cheap field read); (5) the **trip home**, the dearest. Design test: the check costs at most 5 % of the trip home with the item (`C_check <= 0.05 x T_home`), so verifying is the dominant rational line, which is the P4 "costly but possible" shape. A pure phantom pickup (nothing there) is rejected as a glitch read: **a phantom never enters CARRIED** (Boarding F-6, OQ-BS-13). A class-(a) phantom has no subject, is never `lit` and takes no verb (IA-13); only a class-(d) lure, which is real junk, is carried, as itself, and its falsifiers while carried are the weight breath-step, SCAN and the trip home.
*Why.* The object is real, its mass is true, and the lie is falsifiable at a cost lower than the trip home (the concept's P4(d)).
*Edge notes.* Conflict C12 routed to Item DB: IDB:1341 OQ 27 calls the lure-band file "Perception-owned", IDB:333 "Perception and Sanity"; ruling: Sanity owns the data (it is a hallucination rule) and the Filter reads it. The six `# pending referenced_by` entries on Item DB and Suit constants (`weight_class`, `instance_mass_g`, `WEIGHT_MEDIUM_G`, `WEIGHT_HEAVY_G`, `WEIGHT_TELL_MIN_PM`, `STOWED_CUE_FLOOR_CLASS`) were converted to references by the registry stage (C8, closed 2026-10-27).

**SN-22. Class (a), phantom entities and hidden real entities.** *(Deferred: blocked on Horror Entities (Designed, horror-entities.md) and the Perception Filter.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
*Rule.* **Phantoms** exist only in `sanity.perception`. A phantom has no SIM subject, is never registered with Emitter State (EM-21), and has no collision, noise, footprint, occlusion, trail or Attention input. It is drawn only inside the seat's own lit region as a decoy-class overlay within `DECOY_OVERLAYS_MAX` 4 (cited), with `PHANTOMS_MAX[3]` 2 `[PLACEHOLDER]` and the constraint `PHANTOMS_MAX + active decoy lies <= DECOY_OVERLAYS_MAX`. Onset is stage 3 only, `PHANTOM_GAP_S` 60 s apart, on a compartment-entry batch, life `PHANTOM_LIFE_S` 8 to 20 s; intent LURK, STALK or RUSH names *what*, never *how* (the Filter animates). A phantom walks walkable tiles only (a phantom through a wall is a free tell and breaks "geometry never lies", LY-34); it may overlap the seat but never pushes, never raises dust and never collides, which is the falsifier. Phantoms may reach into the cone, close in, and be heard through Sanity's own sound family (SN-25) (INTENSE, user). They may co-occur with real tells and breaches, where they are hardest to separate (the Attention must-consider), and the onset chance is multiplied by `PHANTOM_CO_OCCUR_MULT` 2 `[PLACEHOLDER]` while `phase` is TELL or BREACH, but they never use `web_strand_*`, `vfx_tell_*` or the band slide (AV-18) and never create, replace, delay or fake a tell or a calm (AT-17). The cost of responding is real: running is `NOISE_PER_TICK` 4 against walk 1, which pushes the player toward a breach, so one phantom encounter's expected Attention cost is bounded to a quarter of `ATT_THRESHOLD` (a design bound, so phantoms push toward a breach and never certainly cause one).
**Hidden real entity (HIDE).** A HIDE entry names a real HORROR ref. The entity stays registered (E6), keeps depositing M17 footprints (the web vocabulary, ART 5.5), keeps obeying AT-15 and keeps its own sounds; only its **body is undrawn for that seat** (CON:243 and Horror HE-32: "hides the body, never the trail"). `HIDE_MAX` 1 per seat, life `HIDE_LIFE_S` 8 to 20 s. A HIDE is ineligible or collapses at the next run when the entity is within `HIDE_SAFE_R` 288 px (6 tiles) of the seat, when Attention `phase` is BREACH or SUBSIDING with the entity in the locus, or when `horror.view` reports the entity's state outside {LURK, RETREAT} (STALK is Horror's additive sub-tell, so a hidden horror can never skip its tell). Horror's draft guarantees that outside BREACH or SUBSIDING-in-locus a horror never comes within `LURK_STANDOFF_PX` (HE-11, HE-12, HE-17; range 144..288, default 192 [PLACEHOLDER], registry `LURK_STANDOFF_PX`) of a living seat; `HIDE_SAFE_R` is strictly larger so a hidden thing is never adjacent and closing. **Build assertion:** `HIDE_SAFE_R > LURK_STANDOFF_PX`, evaluated at boot and on every retune of either knob (boot reason `SANITY_BOOT_HIDE_STANDOFF`); at Horror's top of range (288) the default `HIDE_SAFE_R` 288 would fail it, so Horror's retune forces a Sanity retune *(Horror Entities, Designed; status refreshed 2026-10-27 — design review)*. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
*Falsifiers.* The footprint on the floor (a phantom leaves none), the absence of any Attention tell, walking through it (risk), a crewmate, time. **Shadow is not a real-versus-phantom test**: Horror's HE-2 states that a real horror contributes no occluder, casts no shadow, emits nothing and never changes `lux`, which resolves the art bible's 5.4 "occlude the beam, cast shadows" bullet against LO:151 rule 10 and EM-20 (conflict C4: resolved by HE-2 in the Horror draft, Horror's own rows say "C4 resolved by HE-2"; the art-bible bullet refresh is owed to the art-director) and preserves "a shadow with no occluder" as an unambiguous stage-2 sanity tell.
*Why.* The lie costs the player Attention and nerve, not HP; the entity stays a registered, footprint-leaving, tell-obeying thing, so Pillars 1 and 2 hold. Phantoms with SIM presence (they would violate H5 and EM-21) are rejected.
*Edge notes.* A phantom spawned while Attention is CALM leaves `attention.view`, `TellAudit` and `emitters()` unchanged (AC-AT-73; Sanity supplies the double). The lighting decoy kinds `GLOW_EXTENSION` and `PHANTOM_SHADOW` are VS-A content; which the Filter ships, and when, is Sanity's (LO OQ 16). The MVP reaches light only through the lamp-housing module-body channel (SN-16, stage 2).

#### Perception, determinism, audio, co-op and what is never touched

**SN-23. The perception content contract and the first frame.**
*Rule.* `sanity.perception` lists, per seat: `stage`, active lies `{lie_id, slot, subject_ref, kind, direction, reach_tier, params, start_run, active_run, end_run, leak_modes, first_lie, away}` *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: `away` added, G6 GAP)* as typed in the entry schema below (SN-16 lie timing; `leak_modes` always contains GLEAM and the motion bit, SN-15 L-L) and, VS-A, phantom, hide and lure lists. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING: the entry is now a closed schema, see **Entry schema**.)*

**Entry schema (closed; every field is `int`, `bool` or `String`; the Filter reads nothing else).** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING; answers Perception Filter OQ-PF-35 and OQ-PF-38 items 1, 2 and 4.)* The `sanity.perception` entry list is in **ascending `slot` order** and holds at most `LIES_MAX[stage]` entries per seat:

| field | type | definition |
|---|---|---|
| `lie_id` | int >= 1 | minted at the accept from the persisted ledger counter `next_lie_id` (per seat, starts 1, +1 per accept; it is not a draw counter and advances no RNG stream); stable for the life of the lie including its Filter RETURN tail, never reused in a world |
| `slot` | int 0 to `LIES_MAX[3]` - 1 | the lowest free slot at the accept among the seat's live (PENDING or ACTIVE) lies, held until the lie ends; ties impossible (one accept per run per subject, processed in the SN-16 order) |
| `subject_ref` | `{stable_id: String, surface: int}` | `stable_id` is the module's Item/Layout stable id; `surface` is the `lie_targets.json` surface-kind enum, a closed set owned here: 1 NEEDLE, 2 READOUT, 3 GLYPH, 4 GLOW_SPRITE, 5 MACHINE_SPRITE. The inspect card is **not** a separate surface and not a second entry: it is the same instrument as its wall gauge (SN-16 reach, AD-C1), so the Filter draws the card from the NEEDLE or READOUT entry. No WORN-descriptor surface is listed at the MVP (no `PerceptionRef` exists for it, Perception Filter PF-9) |
| `kind` | int enum | 1 FALSE_OK, 2 FALSE_FAIL, 3 GLIDE, 4 SPRITE_SWAP (registry `lie_kinds`) |
| `direction` | int enum | 1 COMFORT, 2 ALARM (L-D), as stored at the accept; immutable for a `lie_id` while the lie lives (a re-derivation that disagrees INVALIDATES it, SN-16); the Filter latches its GLIDE side from it (Perception Filter D.3) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, answers Perception Filter blocker 2)* |
| `reach_tier` | int 1 to 3 | the lowest stage at which the surface is legal (NEEDLE and READOUT 1, GLYPH and GLOW_SPRITE 2, MACHINE_SPRITE 3), derived from `surface`, so the Filter never re-derives reach |
| `params` | Dictionary, closed key set per kind | FALSE_OK, FALSE_FAIL and GLIDE on a NEEDLE or GLYPH: `{}` (a GLYPH, GLOW_SPRITE or static MACHINE_SPRITE carries no motion param: the Filter draws BREATH, or the rotor drift, from `surface`, PF-15) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; on a READOUT: `{readout_mode: int}` (1 TICK, 2 STALE; the numeral's motion mode, Instruments C2), defined as a pure function of the kind: GLIDE gives 1 TICK, FALSE_OK and FALSE_FAIL give 2 STALE (Perception Filter PF-18); it consumes no draw (the draw count per accepted onset stays 4, SN-24); a NEEDLE entry's card numeral follows the same map without a param *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, answers Perception Filter blocker 2)*; SPRITE_SWAP: `{frame_id: String}` (the machine's own honest alternate frame). Values are `int`, `bool` or `String` only; a `float`, `Vector2`/`Vector2i`, `Node`, `Object`, `Callable`, nested Dictionary or Array is a lint failure (`SANITY_BOOT_LIE_TARGET_DATA` for the data file, AC-SN-136 at runtime) |
| `start_run`, `active_run`, `end_run` | int | absolute `run = T div 12` (SN-1 units), `active_run` published (OQ-PF-17), `end_run` counted from activation |
| `leak_modes` | int bitmask | non-empty, GLEAM (1) and bit 2, the surface class's motion mode (L-L), always set *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* |
| `first_lie` | bool | true on the entry accepted while `lies_seen` was 0 |
| `away` | bool | true iff the subject's compartment (the Graph cid holding the module's anchor node) differs from `cid(P)` at this run, else false; a pure function of ledger and `cid(P)`, rebuilt every run and in `post_restore`; read only by the `reach_lie_active` predicate and the Filter's rim gate *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G6 GAP, supersedes the 2026-10-27 delegated G1 listing rule)* |

**Which entries are listed.** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G6 GAP, supersedes the 2026-10-27 delegated G1 listing rule.)* Every ACTIVE entry of the seat is listed, whichever compartment its subject sits in; entries whose subject is not in `cid(P)` carry `away` true. An away entry keeps its clock, its `lie_id` and its slot, and leaves `sanity.perception` only on a real end (EXPIRED, DEBUNKED, INVALIDATED), identical to an entry the seat is standing beside. PENDING entries are not listed. The `stage` field is always listed. Reason: a removal on leaving would make the Filter run its RETURN on every lie visible through an open door or at far zoom, a reliable "walk out of the room; if the gauge settles it was lying" test and an end-of-lie signature (SNV-11); the Filter must not be able to distinguish an away flip from nothing, and it draws only surfaces that are on screen and lit. This closes OQ-SN-17 (and Perception Filter's "away entry" question) on the Sanity side. The fringe oracle is closed by the predicate, not by hiding the entry. **Reach-lie predicate (OQ-SN-21):** `reach_lie_active(P)` is true iff the seat's `stage >= 2` and a **listed** entry with `away` false and `reach_tier == 2` has stepped PENDING to ACTIVE (a PENDING lie is not listed at all); the Filter evaluates it on the listed entries only, so it is a pure function of `sanity.perception`. Away entries and `reach_tier` 3 entries never drive the fringe. **Diagnostic accessor** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, Debug Overlay blocker 1)*: `ledger_listed(player_id, lie_id) -> bool` is true iff `sanity.perception` for that seat holds an entry with that `lie_id` in the current Sanity run, i.e. the row is ACTIVE (away or not); false for PENDING rows, ended rows and an unknown id. It is a pure function of SIM state, derived (not persisted, not a ledger field, not in the SHAPE key set, no `schema_version` change, not hashed), rebuilt in the same hook step that rebuilds `sanity.perception` (SN-2 step 6, after the state transitions and the `cid(P)` evaluation, so the two always agree) and in `post_restore`. The only permitted reader is the Debug Overlay (DIAGNOSTIC); no sim system reads it. An away row is listed, so the Filter holds an entry for it and the Overlay's join sees no skew. **Leak mask:** Sanity stores the full `leak_modes` mask in the ledger; the Filter's plausibility drop of the SOUND bit at stages 2 and 3 (PF-15) is a pure `rng_derive` keyed `"filter.leak/<PlayerId>/<lie_id>"` (the Filter's stream name, a proposed registry stream) that advances no counter and touches no ledger field, so the SIM state is identical with the Filter on or off, and the published mask is never reduced below two modes (L-L). **Legal-kind additions (OQ-PF-38 item 3).** The SN-16 legal-kind set, before `sum_W`, also removes a kind whose pre-draw eligibility fails for the Filter: (a) the subject's dial leaves less than `D_VIS_MIN_DEG` plus the wander margin of room on the side of the lie's `direction` (the dial-room gate) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, answers Perception Filter blocker 2: was "in some direction")*: for a GLIDE the room is measured on the dial side `s = d x c_dial` (`d` +1 COMFORT, -1 ALARM; `c_dial = sgn(mid(OK) - mid(A))` from the authored OK and alarm intervals of the subject's `lie_targets.json` row, the rule of Perception Filter D.3), so a GLIDE whose only room is the wrong way is refused without a draw; for FALSE_OK and FALSE_FAIL gate (b) already covers the zone side; (b) for FALSE_OK and FALSE_FAIL, the zone-edge distance plus `m_z` exceeds `D_MAX_FRAC_ARC x 1000` (the zone-edge gate); both read the Filter's registered constants and the **authored** dial range and zone intervals in `lie_targets.json` for the subject's true-state class (HEALTHY, DEGRADED, FAILED, which Sanity already reads from `hp`), never `true_value()` (the Interactions row keeps that read the Filter's alone), and both are no-draw gates (a refusal advances nothing). They are a provisional contract until the Filter confirms the constants (R9); the Filter's `lie_short_reach` and `lies_zone_missed` counters remain the safety net. It states **WHAT and WHEN**, never HOW (no pixels, shaders, alphas, easing). Every entry carries a non-empty leak (SN-15 L-L). It is rebuilt **in full** every run and in `post_restore` (CR-8: `persist false` means derived, rebuilt after load). Lint: no symbol of `sanity.perception` or `sanity.ledger` is read outside `sanity/` except, respectively, the Perception Filter / replication adapter and the Debug Overlay (DIAGNOSTIC); `ledger_listed` is read only by the Debug Overlay (DIAGNOSTIC) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. H5 (KRN:125) and CR-12 assert it. `post_restore` rebuilds from the restored `sanity.state` and `sanity.ledger` (`restore_after` places the ledger after the Graph and Layout blocks so subject refs resolve, SL-15 item 8); a **malformed** ref, or any out-of-range or wrong-shape value, is a hard `SANITY_RESTORE_*` failure and is never clamped. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review, BLOCKING: a well-formed `subject_ref` that no longer resolves is **not** corruption and is not a failure. Sanity runs only when `T mod 12 = 0` while Save/Load snapshots at any tick boundary (SL:453), so a subject destroyed or salvaged between hook runs and followed by an autosave leaves a legitimate dead ref in a valid save; the MVP death rule (restart from the last autosave) would otherwise hard-fail on it. `post_restore` therefore does three things and mutates nothing persisted: it restores the ledger exactly as stored; it builds `sanity.perception` from the ledger **exactly as the uninterrupted run holds it at that tick**, the dead-ref entry included, so the projection stays field-for-field equal (the Filter draws nothing for a subject that no longer exists, and an unresolved ref answers like unlit, EM-21); and the next hook run's ledger-upkeep step (SN-2 step 4) INVALIDATES the entry with no immunity and prunes its `visit`, `evented[]`, `debounce[]`, `immune[]` and `repaired_recent[]` entries, exactly what the uninterrupted run does. Pruning inside `post_restore` is rejected because `state_hash` would then differ between a restored and an uninterrupted run for up to 11 ticks, which breaks AC-SN-76's every-tick equality. Sanity has no destruction callback and none is requested of the Graph or Layout.)*
*Why.* This answers SL H4, "no clean frame": one unfiltered frame would be a free oracle. Sanity claims **co-ownership of SL OQ-11 with the Perception Filter and the Pipeline** (C9) and states its half: the perception data is ready before the first RUNNING frame (AC-SL-93).
*Edge notes.* The soft cycle Sanity to Filter (IDX:267) resolves by one-way ownership: Sanity writes only perception state; the Filter writes only `CanvasItem` and material properties and never calls back. The Filter's per-stage onset smoothing is its own, subject to PR-13 (at most 3 flashes in a rolling 1 s) and "no uncaused pixel motion". **Ended entries are not retained** (Perception Filter OQ-PF-9): an ended lie leaves `sanity.perception` on the same run (SN-16), so loading clears the Filter's presentation-local glide-return tail; the first RUNNING frame after a restore shows the true value behind the black hold with no lie active to hide, so it is not a clean-frame oracle, and the residual is accepted.

**SN-24. Determinism, restore and no reroll oracle (answers SL OQ-37).**
*Rule.* Every draw is `rng_derive(world_seed, "sanity.<stream>/<PlayerId>", counter)` (for the lie stream the key is `"sanity.lie/<PlayerId>/<stable_id>"` with one counter per subject) or `rng_int(lo, hi)` (the kernel's D.10 functions; no other RNG exists) with the counter inside `sanity.ledger`, persisted and restored exactly (SL H2). Counters advance only on player-driven events: a gated lit-entry event on that subject for `sanity.lie`, a pickup or lit-entry for `sanity.lure`, a compartment-entry batch for `sanity.phantom` and `sanity.hide`. Targets come from the live lit set. Draw consumption is fixed per outcome (0 when any pre-draw gate refuses, including an empty legal-kind set; 1 on a rejected onset; 4 on an accepted one, D.5), so a replay is bit-exact. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the "0" case is the legal-set gate, SN-16.)* **No content depends on `restore_reason`** (SL-24; REG:93-101: no consumer uses `DEATH_STUB` to alter hallucination content), and the level rolls back with the world (SL H3). Quit and death therefore offer no reroll: restore is exact for the same actions. **Accepted residual** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED: the earlier same-moment replay claim contradicted X8, below: the lie draws are per-subject and replay exactly, but the Attention phase and tier that D.1 reads re-key per restore epoch, so the level, the stage, the gap and the cap, and so the moment a draw is consumed, differ after a death)*: identical actions after a death draw the same per-subject dice sequence (the same outcome for the same visit of the same subject), not the same moment, priced by a death, as the scrubber that fails at minute four is in the physical world (Pillar 1). **No memorised script** (SL OQ-37's second constraint): the replay is identical only for identical actions (route, timing, beam and the lit set); any deviation re-gates every draw (gap, cap, stage, immunity, debounce), and a remembered lie is still a lie the ladder prices, so repeated deaths buy foreknowledge of one moment, never a fixed hallucination script. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings: X8. Attention's and Horror's threat draws re-key per restore epoch and are not steerable by quit or death (Save/Load OQ-37); Graph physical failures and persisted counters replay exactly. Sanity's lie, lure, phantom and hide draws are not threat draws: they stay keyed on `world_seed`, the PlayerId and the persisted per-subject counters, and replay exactly, as written above. The restore epoch is Save/Load's counter and is not `restore_reason`, so "no content depends on `restore_reason`" still holds. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R1 as amended by R13/R14:* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R13/R14)* *Save/Load owns `restore_epoch` as a persisted u32 in its per-slot side file `slot_epoch.bin`, advanced on a death-stub restore and on a Continue of any generation other than the newest Save & Quit (`QUIT`) generation, which carries it unchanged, and durable before `request_restore` (delegated ruling R1 as amended by R13/R14); the kernel exposes it read-only in `kernel.clock` and owns the single threat-draw derivation (`counter_eff = rng_counter + RESTORE_KEY_STRIDE x restore_epoch`) that Attention and Horror call. **Sanity reads neither the epoch nor `restore_reason`:** no Sanity draw, gate or counter takes the epoch as input, so the Sanity determinism claims (AC-SN-76) are defined given the save bytes alone, and the only epoch-dependent input to a Sanity run is the Attention `phase` and `tier` it reads.)* Because the threat draws around a restored seat differ, the Attention phase and tier that D.1 reads can differ after a death even though the lie draws do not.)*
*Why.* It satisfies SL H2, the registry rule and G3 / G4 (KRN:123, D.12) and is fully testable. Re-keying after restore is rejected: keyed from `restore_reason` it breaks SL-24; keyed from a PROFILE epoch counter it needs a PROFILE block while zero ship in the MVP (SL:285); and any re-key triggered by quit-and-Continue is a steerable reroll.
*Edge notes.* Per-subject visit counters (checkpoint CQ2, closes OQ-SN-14 for this GDD) mean no unrelated lit-entry elsewhere can shift a subject's dice: a deliberate pre-burn in the next room changes nothing, which removes the steering oracle, the clause of SL OQ-37 that matters. The residual is that "this subject lies on its third visit" is a per-seed fact, priced by a death (SL H3, the accepted residual above) and different in every world; the realised lie still varies with route and timing because the gates (gap, cap, stage, immunity, debounce) are dynamic. The counter-keying choice stays an ADR candidate. The engine reference has no scripting or core module file; GDScript integer overflow, signed-shift behaviour and Dictionary iteration order are **[UNV]**, so the ledger snapshot and `state_hash` must iterate sorted keys, and any `visit_key` or counter must stay below 2^53 (SL S-1). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: engine-level hazards that the determinism lint also bans, AC-SN-130: `get_instance_id` (differs between runs), `hash` (the engine's `hash()` is not a stable cross-version contract), `shuffle` and `pick_random` (they draw from the global RNG), `Array.sort_custom` and `sort` on keys that can tie (the sort is not stable; every sort here carries a total key ending in `stable_id`), and iteration over an unsorted `Dictionary` or `Set`.)*

**SN-25. The audio authorisation.**
*Rule.* Sanity authorises a **hallucination sound family** (the reading-lie sound signature of HR-5; phantom movement), owned and mixed by Adaptive Audio & Tells, disjoint from the tell family, the Graph loop sounds and Horror's sub-tell. Consequence-channel sounds, the human voice and the P1 proximity cue are never altered or faked. Adaptive Audio answers whether audio can lie (AA-1, AA-35: world sounds are ground truth, except the single `hal` family); any audio corruption is within the Filter's write set under the same rules.
*Why.* HR-5 requires a sound signature on every lying reading (ART:976). Adaptive Audio & Tells is designed (`adaptive-audio-and-tells.md`) and a hard edge in the systems index (IDX:202).

**SN-26. Co-op: per-seat independence (VS-B, designed, dormant).**
*Rule.* Per-seat levels are independent. The replicated summary is `{stage, tell_on, harm_eligible}` only; the ledger is HOST_ONLY; perception is OWNER_PRIVATE. No sanity appears on any crew strip or HUD surface (IDX:52). A saner crewmate aids recovery (SN-8) and is the third rung of the ladder. Voice, chat and beam are never altered. Everything is keyed by PlayerId, never `peer_id` (KRN:52, 100). Transport, validation and the replication adapter are Co-op's. A seat rebinding keeps its stored state (SN-3).
*Why.* The victim must see the attacker's tell (KRN:202) without any client learning another client's lies.

**SN-27. What Sanity never touches (a closed list, restated as a lint list).**
*Rule.* `body.state` and positions; walls, door leaves, seams and every geometry (LY-34, MSA F1); trails and Failure & Trail output; every consequence-channel item (SN-15 L-C); Attention outputs, thresholds and `TellAudit`; prompts, durations and the gate order; save and pause surfaces and autosave timing (SL H5, H6); camera and controller values (PCI:42, PIPE C1 and C9: no movement, aim or channel value reads sanity; identical input at stages 0 to 3 gives byte-identical `pos`, `facing` and `aim_angle`); the human channel; emitters (`emitter.state` has no phantom entry, AC-AT-73); Suit damage logic, the limiter and the body cues; and the pixels of any trail, which are bit-identical with the Filter on or off (FT-2, AC-88).
*Why.* This is the single place a reviewer checks that a new feature did not smuggle in a stat penalty or a consequence-channel lie. Each item is also an AC in Acceptance Criteria.

**SN-28. Data flags and QA controls.**
*Rule.* `CLASSES_ENABLED` is a data bitmask (a = 1, b = 2, c = 4, d = 8); the MVP value is {b}.
