### INTENSE (user decision) versus "Doubt, not horror" and "Energy unchanged"

The tension is real and is resolved in one rule: **intensity is delivered through content, never through screen energy.** Permitted levers: lie count (`LIES_MAX` 1 / 2 / 4), lie kind (comfort kinds weighted early, fright kinds rising at stage 3), reach (needle to glyph, card and glow sprite to whole sprite), phantom proximity and motion word (VS-A), monsterised crewmates (VS-B). Forbidden levers: tempo changes in motion or music, screen shake, blur, chromatic aberration, vignette or tunnel (SE-27), scan-line or glitch (7.3's rejected alternative: "the HUD is broken" versus "the gauge is wrong"), global desaturation (4.4's rejected alternative), heartbeat pulse, any global colour grade, any HUD change (HR-13). A player must never read their sanity off the screen's energy instead of off the world (2.9). The frightening image is a needle that disagrees with the player's own breath, not a screen that shakes.

### Animation and motion constraints

| ID | Constraint | Source |
|---|---|---|
| SNV-23 | **Exactly three HUD and instrument motion signatures; only one belongs to a lie.** Settle (honest change: damped spring 250 to 400 ms, overshoot at most 5 percent, then still), tremor (honest stress: plus or minus 1 art px, irregular, 6 to 10 Hz, around a TRUE value, rests when the stress ends), glide (the lie: 0.5 to 2 deg/s, slow micro-wander below 0.5 Hz, never rests). A lie never borrows settle or tremor; an honest gauge never glides. The distinction is cause, mass and rest. | ART 7.3 |
| SNV-24 | Honest numerals tick per real change; lying readouts tick continuously or hold stale (tick interval at least 334 ms, default `LIE_TICK_MS` 400; registry `lying_digit_tick_schedule`). The mechanism is always honest; only the value lies. | 7.3, HR-13 |
| SNV-25 | **Reduced motion (HR-8) preserves the lie's backbone:** glide slows but survives ("never-rest": the needle is always a pixel from where it was a second ago) and the continuous digit tick survives, so a lying instrument stays distinguishable from an honest one at rest. Tremor is the one signature that may need a static substitute (accessibility-specialist). The breathing fringe is slow (4 to 6 s) and luminance-smooth; art-director position: it **stays** under reduced motion (stripping it removes the HR-7 motion channel); routed to the accessibility-specialist (OQ-SN-23). | 7.3, HR-8 |
| SNV-26 | **The fringe breathes; it never flickers.** Flicker is the flare, torch and decoy word; steady-then-waning is the glowstick word; strands are beat-locked and discrete. The fringe must not lock to `tell_beat`, the alarm sweep (2 to 3 s, about 50 percent duty) or the voice-activity cue. | 3.2, 5.5, ATT:93 |
| SNV-27 | Stage onset and offset smoothing is the Filter's, bounded by PR-13 (at most 3 flashes in a rolling 1 s), HR-13 (3 Hz) and "no uncaused pixel motion" (PIPE:37). The SIM steps instantly; the picture does not. | SN-4, SN-23 |
| SNV-28 | Phantom and class-(c) rigs use ART 5.4 construction: segment rigs, procedural, stepped at a fixed low frame rate, grid-snapped segment rotation (or redrawn at key angles), joint overlap with no gaps; the 3.1 silhouette test on three canonical poses (rest, reach, move). The class-(c) rig is driven by true velocity; the phantom rig by intent. | 5.4, 5.5 |
| SNV-29 | **Pixel-perfect rendering of the fringes.** The 2 art px fringe (SNV-14) and the rim band (SNV-10) are defined in **art px of the 640x360 world grid**, so at integer zoom k they are 2k screen px with no sub-art-pixel softness. A post-process on the upscaled `SubViewportContainer` would soften the band and break the HD-pixel-art rule; the unlit fringe pass must be drawn **inside the world render at world resolution** (technical-artist to confirm against PIPE PR-10's single `ShaderMaterial` slot; engine reference: 4.7 `CanvasItem` lines lose their AA feather, breaking-changes.md:31). Verify at every zoom ladder step `k` in `[1, k_auto+2]`. | 8.1, 8.2, PIPE |
| SNV-30 | **A lie's end must not be a visible event** (SNV-11). Open: OQ-SN-24 for sprite swaps. | 7.3, design test |
| SNV-31 | **Stage-2 and stage-3 leaks do not move the camera or the HUD.** The HUD `CanvasLayer` is excluded from the per-client hallucination filter and from shake, blur, chromatic aberration and scale change (HR-6, HR-13). Readings move inside a still frame; that stillness is what makes stage-1 drift readable. | 7.3 |

### Art bible principles and sections that apply

Section 1 Corollary and Principles 1 to 3 ("When the Game Lies, the Lie Leaks": every hallucination leaks violet or flesh somewhere, subtle allowed and absent not; the drift order; anchors never shift. Principle 1 true occlusion and the true light radius never lying; Principle 2 state lives in the sprite and the lie extends from gauge to sprite, icon layer rejected; Principle 3 emission is exposure, so a glow-sprite lie must not read as a lantern). 2.7 The Tell (Attention's truth channel; keep the fringe disjoint), **2.9 Low-sanity overlay** (the governing paragraph: doubt not horror, energy unchanged, the carried element is the leak at the edge of the light), 2.10 Crew loss. 3.1 readability contract, 3.2 (class-c rule, victim fringe, hidden-entity occlusion), 3.4 (frame is anchor, reading is instrument; the reading register), 3.5 (a phantom earns no hero shape; the beam stays the brightest shape). 4.0 principles 2 and 4, 4.1, 4.2 (no "safe" colour), **4.4 the sanity palette (the stage table is this system's visual spec)**, 4.5 luminance ladder, 4.6 (violet is never a UI colour except the gleam on a lying reading), 4.7 colour-vision rows. 5.3 (sanity is never drawn on the body; there is no self-tell), 5.4 (what horrors may borrow), 5.5 (the telling-apart table; the victim fringe; footprints), 5.6 anchors per facing. 7.0 (three layers; the inspect card lies exactly as the wall gauge; no sanity meter anywhere), 7.3, 7.5 (crew strip; no roster flag), 7.6 items 9, 13, 17. 8.2 (fringe clearance of 2 px in every character and horror cell), 8.5 (naming; no `ovl_` for sanity), 8.12 (lighting spike). Hard requirements: HR-5, HR-6, HR-7, HR-8, HR-10 (no global hue remap), HR-13, HR-14.

### Palette rules

| Rule | Value or constraint | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Violet (leak) colour | The **fringe** `#B07BE6` (hue 270 to 330 deg, S above 25 percent) for every leak on readings, edges and outlines. **Never the core `#8A3FD1`**: the core is about 2.9:1 on Ground and fails; the fringe is about 5.3:1. | 4.4, 7.6 |
| Violet placement | Only at an **edge**: cone rim, pipe silhouette, gauge glass, sprite fringe. Never a fill, never a plane. | 4.0-4, 4.7 |
| Flesh | `#D9A58C` and `#B5705C` (hue 5 to 30 deg, S 20 to 60 percent, V 45 to 85 percent). **Stage 3 only.** On surfaces and on phantom and lure content. Never on UI, never on a sprite of a real thing, never over a trail. | 4.4 |
| Drift order | Violet before flesh. Violet is a cool "my eyes are off" error; flesh is a warm "the ship is alive in the wrong way" change. No flesh at stages 1 and 2. | 4.4 |
| Stage-2 "apparent warmth" bound | Any warm shift at stage 2 (VS-A decoys) holds **hue at or above 36 deg** (sand and brass, the sane Tungsten-on-steel result), never at or below 30 deg. Asset test: sample Hull Grey and lit Graphite under the shifted light; hue stays at or above 36 deg. | 4.4 |
| Stage-3 flesh versus Tungsten-lit steel | Stage-3 flesh sits in the 30 deg-or-below band; it is not produced by pulling Tungsten toward orange or by desaturating the light ("pull it back toward yellow, never desaturate"). The Filter tints surfaces in the unlit and presentation pass; it never changes the beam colour. | 4.4, 4.7 |
| Colour-only, luminance-preserving | The Filter adds violet and flesh only. Surface tints are luminance-preserving within a tolerance the spike sets; any luminance change counts against PR-13 and HR-13. | PIPE PR-10 |
| No Alarm Red in a lie | A lie never uses the Alarm Red sweep or the alarm sound class (L-A). Gauge zone bands are not red. The ship-alarm and world-housing warning marks are driven only by Graph `alarm_eligible` (true): a lie may neither show nor suppress them; a suit-rung mark inherits its reading's class (HUD HS-30, OQ-SN-36). | 4.1, 4.2, SE OQ-22 |
| No "safe" colour | No green or blue "all clear" for a debunk or a scanner result. | 4.2 |
| Loop hues in a lie | A lying gauge or sprite uses the loop's own hue and fitting silhouette (the lie is wrong in value, not in vocabulary). Stage-3 bleed adds violet **at the silhouette edge** and never replaces the loop hue or changes the flow pattern. | 4.1, 4.2, 3.4 |
| Anchors untouched | Tungsten beam colour and shape (all four player shapes), Chemlight `#86B896`, flare and torch pools, character sprites, hand tools, HUD frame, the true light radius. | Corollary, HR-6 |
| Deeper rings | Iridescence rises with depth and excludes 270 to 330 deg, so a deep ring never leaks violet to a sane player; violet therefore always means the player's own mind. | 4.1, 4.3 |

**Luminance ladder (greyscale and achromatopsia): new collisions to record** (computed by the art-director from the published hexes, sRGB to L*, approximate; the spike verifies; the 4.5 ladder update is owed by the art-director, OQ-SN-28):

| Colour | L* (approx.) | Collides with | Carried by |
|---|---|---|---|
| Violet **fringe** `#B07BE6` | about 61 | Hull Grey about 61, Fuel Ember about 61 | Edge placement and shape (a tangent continuous band at the cone rim, a glass-edge gleam, a 2 px outline fringe) against Hull Grey's raised seam, rib or hairline and Fuel's thick flanged pipe and glossy puddle; the **breathing motion** (4 to 6 s), which neither has |
| Violet **core** `#8A3FD1` | about 43 | Alarm Red about 47, Graphite lit about 43 | Not used for leaks (HR-5: fringe, never core); listed for the ladder only |
| Flesh light `#D9A58C` | about 72 | Chemlight about 70, between Power Amber and O2 (about 80) and Hull Grey (about 61) | Flesh appears on surfaces and phantom content with no cone and no source sprite; Chemlight is an omnidirectional pool with a source sprite and is untouched by sanity |
| Flesh deep `#B5705C` | about 54 | between Hull Grey (about 61) and Alarm Red (about 47) | Surface and phantom placement; never a sweep |

The hue-removed rule (HR-5 test) therefore requires that **motion and edge placement carry the leak with hue gone**: a tester with hue removed must still find the lying instrument above chance by the glide, and the lying rim by the breathing tangent band. The ladder is a backstop, not the primary channel (4.5). Colour-vision rows (4.7): "Violet versus Void Steel and blue shadow" (deutan and tritan read violet as blue): violet only at edges, a sane shadow has an occluder and a lying one does not, backed by physical falsification. "Flesh versus Tungsten-lit steel" (achromatopsia and mild for all): flesh on surfaces and phantom content, Tungsten is a light with a cone, lit steel holds hue at or above 36 deg. HR-10: **no full-frame daltonization**; colour-vision support for sanity leaks is the tri-modal redundancy, not a hue remap.

### Truth-channel versus corruptible classification of each visual

The **leak elements are truth-channel by construction** (a gleam, a fringe or a hue bleed never lies about whether something is lying). The corruptible set is exactly the module-body channel (LS-14, LSG:864); the truth set is the closed consequence-channel list plus the bible's anchors.

| Visual | Class | Note |
|---|---|---|
| Trail VFX: mist, drip, frost, scorch, debris, puddle (`trail_*`) | **TRUTH (consequence channel)** | Bit-identical with the Filter on or off (FT-2, AC-88). Never painted over by stage-3 violet or flesh: **the PR-10 truth mask must exclude trail pixels and consequence VFX from the hue bleed and from the rim fringe.** |
| The `acoustic` classes at a descriptor anchor; HISS, groan TICK, cavitation TICK, relay clunk | **TRUTH** | Never suppressed or altered. |
| Suit body cues: breath step, visor fog, rime, shiver, reserve valve click, load layer, seam jet, HEAVY back-frame, stagger, walk-only | **TRUTH** | Suit's current set (SE-27); not "vignette" (C7). The 5.3 vital overlays are anchors on every client. |
| Lamp brightness from `served_pm`; the floor light pool; `lux` | **TRUTH** | The lamp-housing glow SPRITE is corruptible; the light it appears to emit is not. |
| `AIRFLOW` draught; `SEAL_STRAIN` creak and strain | **TRUTH** | |
| Attention outputs: `web_strand_*`, `vfx_tell_*`, band slide, filaments, tell audio, dust-direction change | **TRUTH** (exclusive to Attention's Presenter, AV-18) | No phantom tell, no false calm, any tier, any stage (AT-17). Sanity assets may not reuse them. |
| Trails and footprints of real entities including HIDE (`vfx_footprint_*`) | **TRUTH** | Hides the body, never the trail. |
| Geometry: wall faces, door leaves, seams, cap plate, bead, sockets, `CUT_BLOCKED`, install prompts | **TRUTH** | LY-34, MSA F1. |
| Anchors: character sprites, hand tools, HUD frame, true light radius, crewmate beam colour, shape and chest origin, voice, chat, speaker indicator, crew-strip tile, menu, pause, settings | **TRUTH (anchor)** | HR-6. |
| Interaction prompts and durations; save and pause surfaces | **TRUTH** | IA-10 to IA-13; SL H6. |
| Ship-alarm and world-housing warning marks; Alarm sweep | **TRUTH** | Driven by Graph `alarm_eligible` only (SE OQ-22). A suit-rung mark inherits its reading's class (HUD HS-30, OQ-SN-36; honest at the MVP). |
| Scanner reading (MVP) | **TRUTH (anchor)** | VS-A: corruptible at stage 3 only, tri-modal. |
| Suit readings: `suit.o2`, `suit.reserve`, `suit.integrity`, `suit.warmth`, `suit.charge` | **Corruptible, architected; HONEST in the MVP** | VS-A order: warmth first (numeral warm, breath cold), then o2 and charge; integrity and reserve last. |
| Pipe emission, flow chevrons, pipe pattern, pipe diameter and fitting silhouette | **TRUTH (art-director classification; routed to the Graph owner since LS-14 omits it, OQ-SN-29)** | Principle 2 and 4.2 (running is emissive flow, dead is Graphite) make the pipe a world-state witness; the shape and pattern channel is also the colour-blind backup (4.7). Stage-3 bleed may tint the silhouette EDGE only. |
| Module-body: needle, readout, state glyph, inspect card, WORN descriptors | **CORRUPTIBLE** | Gleam, motion and sound mandatory. |
| Lamp-housing glow sprite (module-body emissive appearance) | **CORRUPTIBLE** | No `Light2D`. |
| Whole machine sprite (state frame swap) | **CORRUPTIBLE (stage 3)** | Uses the machine's own honest state frames; no trail assets. |
| Decoy overlays (`GLOW_EXTENSION`, `PHANTOM_SHADOW`), phantom rigs, lure dressing | **CORRUPTIBLE / hallucinated content (VS-A)** | Never in `emitter.state`; unlit; violet rim. |
| Class-(c) horror rig on a crewmate | **CORRUPTIBLE sprite**, with the **beam and voice TRUTH** | |
| **The leaks:** gleam, own-cone rim fringe, crewmate-outline fringe, stage-3 hue bleed | **TRUTH by construction** | They cannot be faked or suppressed by any other lie; they are the instrument by which the player tells corruptible from truth. |

### Asset types implied

The 8.5 grammar has no shader category; a `shd` category (shader and material resource) should be added to the 8.5 token table at the next bible maintenance (owed by the art-director, OQ-SN-28), so names below are provisional.

| Asset or requirement | Owner | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Gleam material (violet fringe `#B07BE6` on a lie-capable gauge's glass and needle edge) | technical-artist | Lie-capable instrument art ships a **`_msk` slice naming the glass and needle-edge regions** and the **needle as a separate pivoted sprite or slice** (it must be able to glide). No numerals in the world gauge (3.1). |
| Own-cone rim fringe material (tangent band at the true rim) | technical-artist | Drawn at world resolution (SNV-29); reads the true cone rim; never alters it. |
| Crewmate-outline fringe material (2 px, outside the contour, along both beam-cone edges, unlit pass, no light, no Attention) | technical-artist | VS-B. Per-viewer, never baked. **Every `char_*` and `horror_*` cell keeps 2 px of transparent clearance outside the contour** (8.2), or the fringe clips in the atlas; the horror cells (core 64 and 96 px, segments 32x32 to 64x32) include it because class (c) reuses them. |
| Stage-3 hue-bleed material (violet at pipe silhouettes, flesh on surfaces) | technical-artist | Needs a **silhouette-edge mask on pipe sheets** (`pipe_shared_msk` edge channel) and a **truth mask** excluding `trail_*`, `vfx_tell_*`, `web_strand_*`, `vfx_footprint_*`, `light_*` and anchors (PIPE PR-10). |
| Phantom and class-(c) rigs | Horror Entities art | **Reuse `horror_t0x` segment rigs**; no new sheets. The phantom flesh tint is a shader on the rig, not baked (the real horror shares the art). The class-(c) rig needs a **velocity driver** input. The beam stays on the crewmate's `anchor_chest_<facing>`. |
| HIDE | Horror, Filter | No asset; suppresses the body draw; `vfx_footprint_*` remain (Horror's). |
| Lure dressing (VS-A) | Boarding and Item DB art, Filter | The junk def's own sprite (ids 9000 to 9999) plus the lure shader; needs a **`_msk` outline slice** on junk sprites. No `ovl_` asset (5.3). The lure-band data file is Sanity's (`assets/data/sanity/lure_bands.json`), not an art asset. |
| **SPRITE_SWAP content gate** | In-World Instruments, Derelict Authoring | "A rotor spinning on a stopped machine" and "a housing shown cracked on a whole one" need every **lie-capable `mach_*` sheet to carry BOTH honest state frames** (running and stopped; whole and cracked) as ordinary `state` tokens. A machine whose sheet lacks the opposite frame is ineligible for SPRITE_SWAP (SN-16, AC-SN-152). |
| Lamp-housing glow-sprite frame | In-World Instruments | The housing's emissive-appearance frame pair (lit, unlit), as an unlit overlay frame. |
| Dust-in-beam particles (so a phantom's lack of dust displacement is a visible falsifier) | Failure & Trail, VFX | Already required by 5.5 (shared with Attention's Tell); Sanity only depends on it. |

### Audio (the statement Sanity makes; the audio-director owns content, mix and triggers)

- **Hallucination sound family (SN-25).** Every lying reading carries a **sound signature that begins no later than its glide** (HR-5, ART:976). The family is authorised by Sanity, owned and mixed by Adaptive Audio & Tells, and **disjoint from the tell family, the Graph loop sounds and Horror's sub-tell**. Phantom movement (class a) also uses this family. Audio is designed (`adaptive-audio-and-tells.md`, AA-1, AA-35: world sounds are ground truth except this `hal` family).
- **Never altered or faked:** consequence-channel sounds (HISS, groan TICK, cavitation TICK, relay clunk, acoustic classes at a descriptor anchor), the human voice and chat, the P1 proximity cue (physically real, plays on every client), and the tell audio. Whether audio can lie at all is Audio's to state (routed, AC-SN-114).
- **Mood the sound must match:** **doubt, not horror** (2.9): wrong, warm where it should not be, fringed, over-reaching. No heartbeat, no tempo change, no sting at a stage boundary or at a debunk.
- **Silent where required:** the class-(c) victim fringe is silent (ART 5.5); a debunk, a recovery and a stage transition carry no sound.

### Conflicts and flags carried

AD-C1 (inspect card reach) is **resolved** in SN-16 (the card is the same instrument and lies from stage 1). AD-C2 (the lie's end) is **resolved** in SN-16 (glide-return, identical for all end types, SNV-11). C4 (the bible's 5.4 "occlude the beam, cast shadows" bullet versus LO:151 rule 10, EM-20, Horror HE-2): the art-director accepts Horror HE-2; the bible refresh is owed by the art-director and not made here; cost recorded for playtest: a real horror in the beam with no shadow must still read as present (dust displacement, footprints, iridescence). Open items are in Open Questions (OQ-SN-21 to OQ-SN-29).

### What must NOT appear (carried into UI Requirements)

No sanity meter, bar, ring, number, icon or colour on any surface, own or a crewmate's, including under accessibility settings. No violet or flesh on the HUD frame, menus, pause, settings, chat or crew strip (the only violet in screen space is the gleam on a lying reading's glass). No sanity indicator on the player's own sprite (5.3). No vignette, tunnel, blur, chromatic aberration, shake, scan-line, glitch, global desaturation or colour grade. No verdict icon, card, banner or "you were hallucinating" line; no real-or-fake binary on the scanner. No Alarm Red or "safe" colour in any lie or debunk. No relief cue on recovery. No lie that adds scorch, sparks, mist, drip, frost or puddle. No phantom drawn in the dark. No visual used by Attention (`web_strand_*`, `vfx_tell_*`, band slide). No lag of the class-(c) rig behind the `harm_eligible` bit.

📌 **Asset Spec** — Visual/Audio requirements are defined. After the art bible is approved, run `/asset-spec system:sanity-and-hallucination-rules` to produce per-asset visual descriptions, dimensions and generation prompts from this section (the gleam, rim-fringe, outline-fringe and hue-bleed materials, and the authoring requirements on `mach_*`, pipe, junk and char/horror sheets).

## Game Feel

Sanity has no verb, no button and no resource the player manages, so its feel is not input responsiveness: it is **the speed at which the world answers a doubt, and the evenness with which the game refuses to add energy to a lie.** The feel target is the Player Fantasy line "Your eyes are an instrument you cannot recalibrate", delivered as the anchor moment "The needle that won't hold still" (Player Fantasy, beats 1 to 5). The player's own controls are never touched: identical input at stages 0 to 3 gives byte-identical `pos`, `facing` and `aim_angle` (SN-27, AC-SN-80), so **Sanity adds 0 ms of input latency, 0 px of aim error and 0 percent of speed, channel or damage change at any stage.** All timings below are in sim ticks (60 per second), runs (12 ticks = 0.2 s, 5 per second) and milliseconds at the reference 60 Hz render.

### Feel references

*Beyond the Aquila Rift* (the lie is warmer and softer than the truth), *Pandorum* (the hands-free chest lamp as the player's only truth tool), *Project Zomboid* ("death is a bitch, it happens": the lesson survives, SL H3), and the instrument-room register of Apollo consoles and *Satisfactory* (state lives in the frame and the reading). The feel is **doubt, not horror** (ART 2.9): a quiet, plausible, almost-right wrongness that gives the player time to notice. Rejected for feel: screen shake, vignette, heartbeat, tempo ramps, jump scares and any sanity bar (Player Fantasy, reference techniques).

### Input-to-feedback chains

There is no input to Sanity; the chains below run from the player's own world actions to the first perceptible consequence.

| Chain | Steps and timing | First perceptible result | Feel intent |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A. Lit entry to the snagging needle (class b)** | The subject is first lit in the seat's visit (any source) -> detected at the next `sanity.drift` run (0 to 12 ticks, at most 0.2 s) -> held in the entry batch window `ENTRY_BATCH_RUNS` 10 runs (up to 2 s) and processed at its close -> gates (stage at least 1, `LIES_MAX`, `LIE_GAP_S` 45 / 25 / 10 s, debounce 20 s, immunity, the legal-kind set) -> onset draw accepted with probability 150 / 300 / 550 per mille -> PENDING for `ONSET_DELAY_RUNS` 10 to 25 runs (120 to 300 ticks, 2 to 5 s, invisible) -> ACTIVE: the entry joins `sanity.perception` in the run it activates -> the Filter starts the glide on the next rendered frame (at most 16.7 ms at the 60 Hz reference; the Filter's rates are frame-rate independent, AC-SN-166) -> the needle departs the true angle at 0.5 to 2 deg/s with the sound signature beginning no later than the glide | The lie appears **4 to 7.2 s after the lamp reveals a gauge in the entry batch** (2 to 5.2 s for a subject first lit after the window), never on the frame of entry *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: batch window added)* | The glance that snags: the delay decouples the lie from the player's own step, so it reads as the gauge drifting, not as the game reacting to them. No announcement. |
| **B. The snag to the choice** | The glide is slow (a 10-degree disagreement takes 5 to 20 s); the Suit body cue (breath step, visor mist) is true and arrives on its own clock; the player reads the disagreement | The player sees the price of each rung before paying (D.7) | Quiet competence: the player has time and a priced menu, never a timer popup. |
| **C. Check to certainty (the room rung)** | Walk to the witness (examples: 1.2 s at 144 px, 3.2 s at 384 px, 4.0 s at 480 px at `V_WALK` 2 px/tick) -> HISS, AIRFLOW, mist and frost are true world events with no Sanity involvement | The world answers in its own language; no interface confirms it | The walk to the pipe: "a hiss simply is". |
| **D. Check to certainty (the scanner rung)** | Equip (`T_EQUIP` 0 ticks, Suit SE-11) -> lit channel `FIELD_CHECK_TICKS` 120 = 2.0 s (Interaction's linear bar, TOOL noise) -> `record_falsification` from `interaction.step` on the completion tick (never from `apply`, IA-30) -> DEBUNKED stamped on that tick *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* -> the next `sanity.drift` run (0 to 12 ticks) rebuilds `sanity.perception` without the entry -> the Filter begins the glide-return on the next frame | Total: 156 ticks (2.6 s) lower bound from the decision to scan, plus at most 12 ticks (0.2 s) and one frame before the needle starts back | Quiet certainty: **no sound, flash or mark at completion**; the reading in the scanner frame and the needle quietly returning are the only signs (design test sentence). |
| **E. Stage change** | Level crosses a threshold -> `stage_dwell` has reached 25 runs (5 s) since the last change -> the stage changes inside one run -> the Filter ramps the new leaks in over a duration it owns, bounded by PR-13 (at most 3 flashes in a rolling 1 s), HR-13 (3 Hz) and "no uncaused pixel motion"; art-director target: slower than one fringe breath (more than 4 to 6 s) | A leak that arrives like weather, not like an event | The drift must not announce itself through tempo (ART 2.9); recovery is symmetric and silent (no relief cue). |
| **F. Restore to first frame** | `post_restore` rebuilds `sanity.perception` before the first RUNNING frame (AC-SL-93) | **0 unfiltered frames** after load | No clean-frame oracle (SL H4). |
| **G. Eligibility and the monsterised crewmate (VS-B)** | `harm_eligible` sets (after 150 runs = 30 s below 15000) -> every other seat's sprite is swapped for a horror rig for that actor on the same sim tick; at most 1 rendered frame (16.7 ms) of lag; a hard cut | The render level IS the gate bit | No frame in which a horror-looking crewmate has no harm plate, and none in which a plate sits on a human-looking crewmate (IA-31). |

### Weight, timing and snap targets

| Quantity | Value | Unit | Why it feels like this |
|---|---|---|---|
| Hook cadence | 12 ticks (5 Hz) | ticks | Fast enough that a check is reflected within 0.2 s; slow enough to be free of per-tick cost. |
| Stage dwell | 25 runs (5 s) | runs | The stage never chatters; the picture still smooths over a longer ramp. |
| Lie onset delay (PENDING) | 10 to 25 runs (2 to 5 s) | runs | Decouples the lie from the entry step. |
| Lie life | 40 to 120 s | s | Long enough to need a decision, short enough that waiting is a priced rung. |
| Lie gap by stage | 45 / 25 / 10 s | s | Stage 1 quiet and rare; stage 3 close to constant (the intense register). |
| Reference onset cadence (D.11, reference `rho` 0.025 per s and `N` 4) | one onset every 167.2 / 77.6 / 51.7 s (3.6 / 7.7 / 11.6 per 10 min) | s | The "INTENSE in count" dial; replaced by the measured counters. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: renewal model; was 111.7 / 58.3 / 28.2 s.)* |
| Live lies | 1 / 2 / 4 | lies | Intensity by count. |
| Debounce, immunity | 20 s, 60 s | s | A checked subject is clean long enough to work, short enough that a sweep is not free. |
| Needle glide | 0.5 to 2 deg/s, micro-wander below 0.5 Hz, never at rest | deg/s | The one motion no real instrument makes (ART 7.3). |
| Honest settle | 250 to 400 ms, overshoot at most 5 percent, then still | ms | The honest-change signature a lie never borrows. |
| Honest tremor | plus or minus 1 art px, 6 to 10 Hz, rests when the stress ends | px, Hz | Honest stress; never a lie's. |
| Lying digit tick | at most 3 per second (interval at least 334 ms; default `LIE_TICK_MS` 400 ms, `T_tick = max(LIE_TICK_MS, N_r x 334)`, registry `lying_digit_tick_schedule`) | Hz | Continuous or stale, never a tween; HR-13 ceiling. |
| Fringe breath | 4 to 6 s per cycle (240 to 360 ticks), luminance-smooth, never flicker | s | HR-7 and ART 5.5; not locked to `tell_beat`. |
| Flash clamp | at most 3 flashes in a rolling 1 s; no luminance change faster than 3 Hz | count, Hz | PR-13, HR-13. |
| Class-(c) swap lag | at most 1 rendered frame (16.7 ms), hard cut | ms | IA-31 precondition (1). |
| Scan from where one stands (lower bound) | 156 ticks = 2.6 s (`FIELD_CHECK_TICKS` 120 + reach walk 36) | ticks | Cheap enough to be worth it, dear enough to choose. |
| Walk-or-scan break-even `d*` | 5 tiles (3.75 at the 90-tick floor, 10 at 240; 3.75 and 2.5 tiles with one and two compartment crossings, D.7 *Amended 2026-10-27 — design review*) | tiles | A witness nearer than 5 tiles is cheaper to look at, farther cheaper to scan. |
| Descent lead: stage 1 to `tell_on` | at least 222.4 s at the cap | s | The fairness gate (SN-10). |
| Descent lead: `tell_on` to eligibility | at least 83.4 s (113.4 s with the 30 s dwell) | s | The victim's warning (Pillar 2 in spirit). |
| Stage 1 to eligibility | at least 305.6 s (335.4 s with the dwell) | s | "Slow and costly". |
| Hangover after a sortie | 316.8 s to leave stage 1 (66.8 s to leave stage 2) at a settled ring-1 home, from the ring-1 floor | s | The intended "lies on the home ship" beat. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| Pacing hypothesis | stage 1 after about 315.8 s (5.3 min) aboard in ring 1; stage 2 after 473.8 s; floor after 494.8 s | s | A playtest hypothesis (CQ5). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| Escort home (VS-B) | 173 s at a settled home versus 34.7 min at `REC_IDLE` | s, min | The co-op beat: bring the seat home. |

### What the feel must not do

- **No screen energy.** No shake, blur, chromatic aberration, vignette, scan-line, glitch, global desaturation, heartbeat pulse or tempo change at any stage; the HUD layer is excluded from every sanity effect (SNV-31).
- **No input feel change.** No haptic, controller rumble, camera, zoom, time-scale or audio-mix change keyed to sanity. The opt-in haptic pulse on Attention beats for a deaf or muted player in the dark (X10) carries Attention's own information and the player's own true body cues, never sanity, so it is not a sanity-keyed haptic *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. The camera and controller never read it (PCI:42, PIPE C1, C9).
- **No announcement.** No stage sting, no debunk chime, no relief cue on recovery, no "you were hallucinating" line, no cut to the truth.
- **No snap.** A lie neither jumps onto a gauge nor jumps off it: it glides in and glides out, identically for EXPIRED, INVALIDATED and DEBUNKED (SNV-11).
- **No free reading of the level.** Nothing the player sees may let them read their own number: the own-cone rim fringe is lie-bound by the user ruling on OQ-SN-21, so it is drawn only while a stage-2 reach lie is ACTIVE and no exception remains *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review.)*

### Feel risks and the playtest dials

- **Too many lies at stage 1.** The reference rate gives a lie ACTIVE about 48 percent of the time at stage 1 (D.11, re-derived 2026-10-27 — design review; the first model said 72 percent), still a lot for "subtle, gauge-only". Dial: `ONSET_PM` toward the alternative 66 / 128 / 191. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **A stage 3 that feels like a stat penalty.** If lies become constant noise the player stops checking and starts ignoring; dial `LIE_GAP_S` and `LIES_MAX`, never a louder lie.
- **A check that feels free.** At two or fewer eligible surfaces a scan-everything habit is dominant (D.12); the advisory authoring floor is OQ-SN-11.
- **A debunk that feels like nothing.** Quiet certainty is the intent; if testers do not notice the return, the dial is the Filter's return rate (OQ-SN-24), never a verdict cue.
- **The slow glide-return.** At 0.5 to 2 deg/s a large discrepancy returns over tens of seconds after a paid scan; a faster Filter-owned return rate is the lever (never a snap, never a settle).
- **The hangover.** 316.8 s of stage-1 lies (66.8 s of stage 2) on the home ship after a sortie that ended at the ring-1 floor is intended *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED: was 167 s, the figure from the old 62000 floor)*; if it reads as unfair, the lever is `REC_SETTLED`, not a recovery cue.

## UI Requirements

**Sanity & Hallucination Rules has no UI of its own.** It owns no screen, menu, HUD element, widget, prompt or icon, and the player never sees a sanity number, bar, ring, icon or colour (IA-39: "no sanity meter or number exists"; ART 5.3, 7.0; SN-14, G8). Every surface it touches is owned elsewhere, and this section states only what Sanity requires of each owner.

| Surface | Owner | What Sanity requires | Test |
|---|---|---|---|
| In-world gauges, state glyphs, lamp-housing glow sprites, machine sprites | In-World Instruments & Inspect View; Perception Filter | Lie-capable instruments ship the art authoring requirements (separate pivoted needle, `_msk` glass and needle-edge slice, both honest state frames); the Filter draws each lie with its HR-5 tri-modal leak (SNV-7 to SNV-9). | AC-SN-122, 136, 149, 152 |
| Inspect card | In-World Instruments & Inspect View; Perception Filter | The card is the same instrument as its wall gauge and lies exactly when the gauge lies, from stage 1; its frame is an anchor and violet appears only on the reading's glass (SNV-8). | AC-SN-151 |
| Scanner reading and `field_check` | Interaction System; Suit & Equipment (slot 3) | An honest reading in the reading register; never a verdict icon, real-or-fake binary, Alarm Red or safe green (SNV-12). The bar is Interaction's linear channel bar. | AC-SN-72, 147 |
| The one-bit harm prompt (VS-B) | Interaction System | A prompt or an immediate `NOT_ELIGIBLE` is a one-bit statement about the actor's own sanity relative to the harm threshold (IA-31), the only hard self-knowledge Sanity allows. | AC-SN-38, 92 |
| HUD, crew strip, the pause panel, the chart and the main menu (Chart Table), settings, chat | HUD & Suit Layer; Chart Table, Web Map & Menus; Co-op Session | **Nothing about sanity.** The only violet in screen space is the gleam on a lying reading's glass. A crewmate's sanity appears only as the world mark in the world (the class-(c) fringe, VS-B), never on a roster flag. | AC-SN-83, 146 |
| Suit readings and body cues | HUD & Suit Layer; Suit & Equipment | All five readings are honest in the MVP (SE-29); the VS-A per-key switch is the Filter's; body cues and the ship-alarm and world-housing warning marks are never corruptible; a suit-rung mark inherits its reading's class (HS-30, OQ-SN-36). | AC-SN-72, 109 |
| Debug Overlay | Debug Overlay | A DIAGNOSTIC reading of `sanity.state` and `sanity.ledger` (true value beside perceived) and of `ledger_listed(player_id, lie_id)` (SN-23) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, the telemetry counters and the per-seat `forced` flag; `force_stage` and `force_level` are reached from the dev console and displayed by the Overlay (display only, DO-1, OQ-DO-11); never a player surface, never an accessibility feature, absent from release exports by file exclusion (`overlay_build_gate`, `overlay_source_root`). | AC-SN-85, 144 |
| Accessibility | accessibility-specialist; Perception Filter | Colour-vision support is the tri-modal redundancy (gleam, the surface class's motion mode, sound) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, not a hue remap (HR-10); reduced motion keeps the glide's never-rest backbone and the breathing fringe (SNV-25). | AC-SN-149, OQ-SN-23 |

**What must NOT appear on any UI surface:** a sanity meter, bar, ring, number, icon or colour, own or a crewmate's, including under accessibility settings; violet or flesh on the HUD frame, menus, pause, settings, chat or crew strip; a sanity indicator on the player's own sprite; a vignette, tunnel, blur, chromatic aberration, shake, scan-line, glitch, global desaturation or colour grade; a verdict icon, card, banner or "you were hallucinating" line; a real-or-fake binary on the scanner; Alarm Red or a "safe" colour in any lie or debunk; a relief cue on recovery; any Attention visual (`web_strand_*`, `vfx_tell_*`, the band slide) reused by Sanity. The full list is in Visual/Audio Requirements ("What must NOT appear").

**Screens touched, for the screenshot evidence.** At stage 0 and at forced stage 3 (`force_stage`): the HUD, the crew strip, the pause panel, the chart and the main menu (Chart Table), an inspect card on a lying gauge and the scanner reading (no inventory screen exists, CT-37, and no death screen); retained in `production/qa/evidence/` (AC-SN-67, 83, 147). If a future surface gains a real sanity-related control, that is a UX Flag for the owner of the screen; none exists today.

## Cross-References

Every explicit dependency on another GDD's mechanic, value or rule. Nature is one of: Data dependency (we consume their output), State trigger (their state change triggers our behaviour), Rule dependency (our rule assumes theirs is also true), Ownership handoff (we hand a value to them). Entries marked *(provisional)* depend on a neighbour with no GDD or one in flight (a draft with `Status: In Design`).

#### Registry names this GDD introduces (proposed; the registry stage writes them from the final text, reflexion P-11)

Relation, not a knob: `SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR` equals the kernel divisor 12 (KRN:54, KRN:202; the `SUIT_HP_FULL` precedent). Constants (every value `[PLACEHOLDER]`): `SAN_MAX`, `STAGE_ENTER[1..3]`, `STAGE_HYST`, `STAGE_DWELL_RUNS`, `SAN_TELL_ON`, `SAN_TELL_OFF`, `SAN_HARM_ENTER`, `SAN_HARM_EXIT`, `SAN_HARM_DWELL_RUNS`, `DRIFT_MAX_PER_RUN`, `REC_SETTLED`, `REC_IDLE`, `REC_CREW`, `REC_MAX`, `PENDING_MAX`, `SAN_STAGE1_TO_TELL_MIN_S`, `SAN_TELL_LEAD_S`, `SAN_MIN_ONSET_TO_HARM_S`, `SAN_FLOOR_TO_HARM_MIN_S`, `ENTRY_BATCH_RUNS`, `T_CROSS_PROV` (design-time), `RING_EXPOSURE[1..3]`, *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: three names added; the registry stage writes them from this text)* `ABOARD_EXPOSURE`, `AMBIENT_FLOOR[1..3]`, `AMBIENT_MARGIN`, `EXP_TELL[1..3]`, `EXP_BREACH`, `EXP_SUBSIDING`, `EXP_BREACH_ENTRY`, `EXP_HORROR`, `HORROR_NEAR_R`, `HORROR_ENC_BUDGET`, `HORROR_ENC_RESET_S`, `HORROR_TRACK_MAX`, `CREW_R`, `WITNESS_HOPS_MAX`, `HULL_GUARD_MARGIN_BP` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING)*, `LIES_MAX[1..3]`, `LIE_GAP_S[1..3]`, `LIE_LIFE_S`, `ONSET_PM[1..3]`, `ONSET_DELAY_RUNS`, `REPAIR_BIAS_S`, `REPAIR_BIAS_W`, `KIND_W[kind][stage]`, `DEBUNK_IMMUNE_S`, `LIT_ENTRY_DEBOUNCE_S`, `LETHAL_FAULT_MIN_STAGE`, `SECOND_INSTRUMENT_MIN`, `INCIDENTS_MAX`, `PHANTOMS_MAX[3]`, `PHANTOM_GAP_S`, `PHANTOM_LIFE_S`, `PHANTOM_CO_OCCUR_MULT`, `HIDE_MAX`, `HIDE_LIFE_S`, `HIDE_SAFE_R`, `LURES_MAX[1..3]`, `LURE_FRACTION_MAX_PM`, `FIELD_CHECK_FLOOR_TICKS`, `SCANNER_LIE_STAGE`, `READING_LIE_STAGE[key]`, `CLASSES_ENABLED`. Block ids `sanity.state`, `sanity.ledger`, `sanity.perception`; hook `sanity.drift`; mutators `record_falsification`, `record_harm_event`; queries `actor_sanity_below_harm_threshold`, `sanity_view`, `ledger_listed` (DIAGNOSTIC only; SN-23) and the `sanity.perception` entry field `away` (SN-23) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; the debug-only, non-persisted counter `suit_read_stub_hits` (Suit row, AC-SN-182) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G1/G6, creative-director escalation)*; formulas `sanity_drift_step`, `stage_of`, `harm_gate`, `drift_lead_invariant`, `ambient_floor_invariant`, `harm_hysteresis_margin`, `lie_onset`, `lure_dressing`, `verification_cost_ladder`, `horror_encounter_budget`, `lie_rate_estimate`, `immunity_sweep_ratio`, `phantom_response_bound`; boot reason `SANITY_BOOT_LEAD_INVARIANT` (and the rest of the closed `SANITY_BOOT_*` and `SANITY_RESTORE_*` sets). **Cited, never re-registered:** `TICK_RATE`, `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS`, `HP_OK`, `HP_FAIL`, `W_MIN_WARNING_S`, `FIELD_CHECK_TICKS`, `HARM_CHANNEL_TICKS`, `HARM_TOTAL_BP`, `HARM_BP_PER_STEP`, `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER`, `SUIT_HP_FULL`, `DECOY_OVERLAYS_MAX`, `DECOY_MAX_CH`, `DECOY_LEAK_PX`, `DECOY_LEAK_ALPHA_PM`, `WEIGHT_TELL_MIN_PM`, `ATT_THRESHOLD`, `TELL_MIN_TICKS`, `TELL_MAX_TICKS`, `NOISE_PER_TICK`, `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS`, `STEP_BUDGET_MS`, `REACH_PX`, `V_WALK`, `TILE_PX`.

Owed to Attention: pin "`tell_outcome` resets to NONE at TELL entry" in AT-16 and the TELL row *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED)* |

The Filter conforms (G1, the publisher wins), owns the SOUND-bit plausibility drop as a pure `rng_derive` keyed `"filter.leak/<PlayerId>/<lie_id>"`, and confirms the dial-room and zone-edge constants *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING)*. ## Acceptance Criteria

Format: GIVEN / WHEN / THEN, each criterion self-contained (its inputs are inline; a tester needs neither the formula text nor the knob table). Tags: `(auto)` automated test, `(lint)` source or data scan, `(review)` design or code review, `(manual)` playtest or retained screenshot, `(spike)` prototype needed before it can be written as auto. Ids `AC-SN-n` are append-only: ids 43 (a design-review checklist item, QA-17), 81 (split into 132 and 141) and 112 (retired 2026-10-27 — design review: the `lit_static` path is gone) are retired and no id is renumbered. Each criterion cites the rule (`SN-n`), formula (`D.n`) and edge case (`EC-SN-n`) it proves. Split: **MVP-live** (class b, solo, one seat) first, then **Deferred** with the named blocker, then a gaps list. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

**Gate levels and story types** (from `qa.level` full; `project.yaml` has no `testing` override). Logic and Integration criteria: BLOCKING, tests under `tests/unit/sanity/` and `tests/integration/sanity/`. Visual and UI criteria (AC-SN-67, 83, 147): BLOCKING, with screenshots retained in `production/qa/evidence/`. Config/Data criteria (AC-SN-45, 49, 50, 51, 86, 152): ADVISORY by default; recommendation (a user decision, OQ-SN-16) is `testing.strict.config: true` for the `sanity` data files, because they are the pillar guard rails. Playtest criteria (AC-SN-71 manual half, 88, 148, 165, 168): ADVISORY with sign-off. Config/Data additions AC-SN-160 and AC-SN-161 follow the Config/Data rule above. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED: AC-SN-160 is re-graded **Logic and BLOCKING** (it is the retune guard and every endpoint is a boot test) and its schema half is AC-SN-176, Config/ADVISORY; AC-SN-161 (the real slice binds every lie-capable gauge and yields both lie directions, the MVP's lie supply), AC-SN-86 and AC-SN-152 are re-graded **BLOCKING**, because a slice with no lies or an unguarded data file breaks Pillars 1 and 4; AC-SN-70, 90 and 91 test design-time calculators and are graded **review**, not auto.)* Performance criteria (AC-SN-97, 98, 139): BLOCKING on the reference machines RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK (delegated ruling R12, release export), pending spike measurement there; ADVISORY in headless CI *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*.

**Fixture rule.** Worked-example tests (D.1 cases, D.2, D.3, D.5, D.6, D.11, D.12) run against a pinned fixture `tests/fixtures/sanity/knobs_default.json` holding the default knob values, never against the live `assets/data/sanity/` files, so a legitimate retune does not break them. The boot assertions (D.4, D.8, D.9, the immunity assertion) run against the **live** data and are the retune guard. Probabilistic criteria inject the `rng_int` stub (the kernel D.10 seam) so they are deterministic; no criterion uses a random seed. Every number below is a `[PLACEHOLDER]` default except where marked cited.

**Testability prerequisites** (shift-left, for the lead-programmer): an injectable `attention.view`; read stubs for Graph, Body, Layout, Emitter, Horror and Suit; an injectable `rng_int` seam; a run-granularity tick driver; `force_*` in the dev-only source root (`overlay_source_root`, `src/debug/`), excluded from the Release export by file and registered only through the Debug Overlay's gate (`overlay_build_gate`: file-level exclusion plus gated registration; the gate's feature-tag name is the Overlay's own placeholder and [UNV], and Sanity defines no tag of its own) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Sanity stays scene-tree free (H10), so every Logic criterion runs headless. Suggested files (gdUnit4 `[system]_[feature]_test.gd`): `tests/unit/sanity/sanity_drift_test.gd`, `sanity_stage_gate_test.gd`, `sanity_lead_invariant_test.gd`, `sanity_lie_eligibility_test.gd`, `sanity_lie_onset_test.gd`, `sanity_lie_lifecycle_test.gd`, `sanity_restore_test.gd`, `sanity_forbidden_inputs_lint_test.gd`; `tests/integration/sanity/sanity_slice_falsifiability_test.gd`, `sanity_consequence_invariance_test.gd`, `sanity_graph_attention_interaction_test.gd`.

**A. MVP-live (class b, solo, one seat).** Groups A1 to A10 below.

### A1. Blocks, hook, level, stages (SN-1 to SN-4)

- **AC-SN-1 (auto)**: GIVEN the boot registry WHEN the kernel boots with Sanity registered THEN exactly three blocks exist: `sanity.state` (SIM, PER_PLAYER, persist true, `persist_scope` WORLD, `repl_hint` SHARED_SUMMARY, `restore_after` []), `sanity.ledger` (SIM, PER_PLAYER, persist true, WORLD, HOST_ONLY, `restore_after` [`sanity.state`, `graph.state`, `layout.state`, `horror.state`], `horror.state` provisional on Horror Entities HE-3) and `sanity.perception` (PERCEPTION_OUT, PER_PLAYER, persist false, OWNER_PRIVATE), each with `schema_version` 1 and `supported_versions` [1], and no boot lint fires (`SL_BOOT_SCOPE_MISSING` absent). (SN-1)
- **AC-SN-2 (auto)**: GIVEN a snapshot of each persisted block WHEN it is serialised THEN it contains no `Vector2i`, no `StringName` keys and no derived cache, every counter is below 2^53, and dictionary keys are written in sorted order (GDScript Dictionary iteration order is [UNV], so the test asserts sorted output, not insertion order); and the `sanity.ledger` key set is exactly the SN-1 contents list including `last_onset_run` and `last_cid`, so a payload missing either, or carrying any extra key, fails `SANITY_RESTORE_SHAPE`. (SN-1, SN-24) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING)*
- **AC-SN-3 (auto)**: GIVEN the hook registry WHEN boot resolves ordering THEN `sanity.drift` has divisor 12, offset 0 and `after` = [`graph.propagate.failure` (`tolerates_stale: true`), `attention.step`, `emitter.place`, `body.integrate`, `interaction.step`, `suit.vitals` (`tolerates_stale: true`), `horror.step`], every id resolves, boot emits zero never-co-running warnings for `sanity.drift`, and on a tick with both hooks due `interaction.step` runs before `sanity.drift` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: `interaction.step` added)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: the full seven-id list with the two stale-tolerant edges, SN-2)*; WHEN any one `after` id is replaced by an unknown id THEN boot fails. (SN-2, CR-3)
- **AC-SN-4 (auto)**: GIVEN Sanity booted WHEN the command registry and the input-surface registry are listed THEN Sanity has registered no command, no `INPUT_SURFACE` and no PLAYER-origin entry, and the only writers to `sanity.*` blocks are the hook, `record_falsification`, `record_harm_event` and the debug controls. (SN-2)
- **AC-SN-5 (auto)**: GIVEN one tick with `T mod 12 = 0` and seats 1 and 2 bound WHEN the hook runs THEN an instrumented order log shows seat 1 before seat 2 and, per seat, the seven SN-2 steps in order (inputs; D.1; D.2 and D.3; ledger upkeep; lit-entry events and D.5; perception rebuild; visit-bookkeeping store), with `sanity.perception` rebuilt after steps 3 to 5. (SN-2) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-SN-6 (auto)**: GIVEN NEW_GAME WHEN the first seat binds THEN `level` is 100000, `stage` 0, `tell_on` false, `harm_eligible` false, `pending` 0; GIVEN PlayerId 1 with stored `level` 41000 that unbinds and rebinds THEN it keeps 41000 (no join-to-reset). (SN-3, EC-SN-50)
- **AC-SN-7 (auto)**: GIVEN `stage_dwell` 25 and the following (prior stage, level) pairs WHEN D.2 runs THEN: (0, 70000) stays 0 and (0, 69999) gives 1; (1, 72000) stays 1 and (1, 72001) gives 0; (1, 55000) stays 1 and (1, 54999) gives 2; (2, 57000) stays 2 and (2, 57001) gives 1; (2, 25000) stays 2 and (2, 24999) gives 3; (3, 27000) stays 3 and (3, 27001) gives 2. (D.2, EC-SN-1) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-SN-8 (auto)**: GIVEN stage 0, level 69000 and stored `stage_dwell` 24 WHEN D.2 runs THEN the stage stays 0 and the dwell becomes 25; GIVEN stored `stage_dwell` 25 THEN the stage becomes 1, the dwell becomes 0, and a further change is not possible until the 26th run after the change (the run that reads a stored 25 again). (D.2, SN-3 integer conventions)
- **AC-SN-9 (auto)**: GIVEN (level 69964, stage 0, dwell 25), (level 72001, stage 1, dwell 25), (level 57500, stage 2, dwell 25) WHEN D.2 runs THEN the results are stage 1, stage 0 and stage 1 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*, and no input over levels 0 to 100000 with any prior stage in 0 to 3 yields a stage above 3. (D.2)
- **AC-SN-10 (auto)**: GIVEN scripted descents from level 100000 to 0 at constant net 1, 12, 36 per run, then recoveries at net -1 and -15 per run, then a hold at level 14990 for 400 runs, with the hook run every run WHEN each run is checked THEN `stage` is in 0..3, `harm_eligible` implies `stage == 3` and `tell_on`, `tell_on` implies the level has been below 35000 since it last fell under 30000, and no run changes the stage by more than 1. (Restore and `force_*` paths are excluded here and covered by AC-SN-85, 119 and 120.) (D.2, D.3, SN-10)
- **AC-SN-11 (auto)**: GIVEN a seat in each of UNBOUND, BOUND_ALIVE, DOWNED and DEAD (stubbed from Suit and the binding table) with `level` 50000 and net 10 WHEN 100 runs execute THEN the level is 49000 for BOUND_ALIVE and for DOWNED, and 50000 for UNBOUND and DEAD; the ledger of an UNBOUND seat is retained; for DEAD in the MVP the death stub's restore replaces the frozen state with the autosave's; and GIVEN a seat that goes DEAD at the Suit run on tick T = 6k + 2 WHEN the next `sanity.drift` run occurs (the first T mod 12 = 0 at or after 6k + 3, i.e. 4 or 10 ticks later) THEN Sanity reads the seat as DEAD in that run (frozen ledger: no drift, no onset, no startle credit), `horror.step` has run first on any coinciding tick (T mod 60 = 0), and the result is the same under every hook registration order tested *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: Suit at (6, 2) never co-runs with Sanity, SN-2)*. (SN-2, SN-3, EC-SN-49, EC-SN-50) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED)*
- **AC-SN-115 (auto)**: GIVEN a kernel running 120 ticks with one bound seat and one UNBOUND seat WHEN `sanity.drift` invocations are counted THEN it ran 10 times, at ticks 0, 12, 24, ..., 108, and the UNBOUND seat produced no state change. (SN-2, SN-3)
- **AC-SN-116 (auto)**: GIVEN NEW_GAME and an Attention stub reporting `tell_epoch` 7 and `tell_outcome` BREACHED WHEN the first seat binds and one run executes THEN `level` is 100000, `stage` 0, `stage_dwell` 25, `harm_below_runs` 0, `pending` 0, `last_tell_epoch` 7, both flags false, and `pending` is still 0 after the run (no spurious startle). (SN-3 integer conventions, SN-7)
- **AC-SN-117 (auto)**: GIVEN the replication adapter stub WHEN `sanity.state`'s `SHARED_SUMMARY` projection is serialised THEN it contains exactly the keys `stage`, `tell_on`, `harm_eligible` (no `level`, no counters); `sanity.ledger` is HOST_ONLY and `sanity.perception` OWNER_PRIVATE, and the adapter stub receives no key of either. (SN-1, SN-26, CR-12)
- **AC-SN-118 (auto)**: GIVEN `level` 0 WHEN 160 runs execute with net 5 THEN `level` stays 0, `stage` stays 3, `tell_on` stays true, `harm_eligible` is false through run 149 and true from the 150th run, *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED: the earlier case ran 100 runs and then asserted a bit that needs 150)* with no error and no new state; GIVEN `level` 100000, stage 0 and net -12 THEN `level` stays 100000 and zero `rng_int` or `rng_derive` calls are made. (EC-SN-2, EC-SN-3)

### A2. Exposure, recovery, drift (SN-5 to SN-9; D.1)

Cases 12a to 12g run D.1 for one run with `pending` 0, ring 1, `startle_add` 0 and no horror unless stated.

- **AC-SN-12f (auto)**: GIVEN level 80000, aboard in ring 1, phase BREACH, `pending` 1500 WHEN D.1 runs THEN `E` 48, `base` 47, `draw` 0, `net` 36, `level'` 79964, `pending'` 1500. (D.1)
- **AC-SN-12g (auto)**: GIVEN level 80000, home body in ring 1, phase SUBSIDING, `pending` 1500 WHEN D.1 runs THEN `E` 14, `R` 1, `base` 13, `draw` 23, `net` 36, `pending'` 1477. (D.1)
- **AC-SN-13 (auto)**: GIVEN every combination of `E1` in {0, 20, 28}, `E2` in {0, 6, 12, 20, 28, 14}, `E3` in {0, 20, 40}, `E4` 0, `R` in {1, 12}, `pending` in {0, 1500, 3000}, `startle_add` in {0, 1500} WHEN D.1 runs THEN `net` is in [-15, 36], `level'` in [0, 100000], `pending'` in [0, 3000], and `draw` never raises `net` above 36. - **AC-SN-16 (auto)**: GIVEN `attention.view` stubs for phase CALM, TELL with tier 0, 1, 2, 3, BREACH and SUBSIDING WHEN `E2` is read THEN it is 0, 0, 6, 12, 20, 28 and 14; GIVEN a stub whose `level_pm` is poisoned (set to a sentinel) THEN no result changes and a spy confirms `level_pm` was never read. (SN-7)
- **AC-SN-17 (auto, integration)**: GIVEN `phase` CALM, `tell_outcome` BREACHED with `tell_epoch` 8 and stored `last_tell_epoch` 7 WHEN the hook runs THEN `pending` gains 1500 once and `last_tell_epoch` becomes 8; GIVEN a restore with stored `last_tell_epoch` 8 and the live epoch 8 THEN no second credit and `pending` is unchanged; and GIVEN the full Attention sequence CALM (epoch 8 credited) -> TELL (epoch 9, `tell_outcome` still BREACHED because the stub does not reset it) -> BREACH -> SUBSIDING -> CALM (epoch 9, BREACHED) -> TELL (epoch 10) WHEN the hook runs on every run THEN exactly one credit occurs per completed breach (one at epoch 9's first CALM run, none during TELL, BREACH or SUBSIDING, none at epoch 8's lingering outcome), `last_tell_epoch` is overwritten on each CALM run that sees a new epoch (also after an ABORTED tell, with no credit), and `pending` after the sequence equals 1500 minus the draws. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED: corrected to SUBSIDING-end timing; integration case added.)* (SN-7, EC-SN-32)

- **AC-SN-25 (auto)**: GIVEN the D.1 fixture: ring-1 home body (`aboard` false, so `E1 = RING_EXPOSURE[1] = 0` at every level, whatever the floor), phase CALM and tier 0 (`E2` 0), `E4` 0, `crew_saner` false, `pending` 0, `startle_add` 0, `settled` forced false by a stubbed THIN `band_o2` (so `R = REC_IDLE` = 1 on every run), and the cap at 36; and GIVEN a scripted contact simulation of k horror stubs, each within 288 px: all k enter range together and stay until every `pay_h` is 0 (the contact phase), then all leave and stay out for exactly 600 runs (the gap; the 600th out-of-range run resets every `spent_h` to 0, D.10), and the cycle repeats WHEN it is driven until the first run whose `level'` is below 15000 THEN the 1-based count of whole runs from the start of the script to that run (the contact phase of cycle 1 starts at run 1) is exactly:

  - k = 1 from 43000: run 9053 (cycle 13, contact run 53, `level'` 14993); run 9052 ends at 15012;
  - k = 2 from 43000: run 3917 (cycle 6, contact run 52, `level'` 14993);
  - k = 3 from 43000: run 2816 (cycle 4, contact run 104, `level'` 14968);
  - k = 4 from 43000: run 2050 (cycle 3, contact run 164, `level'` 14982).
  Derivation, all at registry defaults (`EXP_HORROR` 20, `HORROR_ENC_BUDGET` 3000, `DRIFT_MAX_PER_RUN` 36, `REC_IDLE` 1; 'loss' = 70000 or 43000 minus level, and 'below 15000' means loss >= 55001 or >= 28001): k = 1: contact is 150 runs of `net` = 20 - 1 = 19 (2850 loss), the gap recovers 600, so a full 750-run cycle loses 2250. From 43000: 12 full cycles = 9000 runs, loss 27000; the 13th contact needs 19 x j >= 28001 - 27000 = 1001, j = 53 (1007; j = 52 gives 988); 9000 + 53 = 9053, level 43000 - 27000 - 1007 = 14993. From 70000: 24 full cycles = 18000 runs, loss 54000; 19 x j >= 55001 - 54000 = 1001, j = 53; 18053, level 70000 - 54000 - 1007 = 14993. k = 2: the cap binds (E3 40, `net` 36, charge `delta` = 36 - (-1) = 37 split in ascending ref order, 20 to horror 1 and 17 to horror 2) for 150 runs, then horror 2 alone (`net` 19, charge 20 per run, 450 left = 22 runs of 20 and one of 10): contact 173 runs, loss 5827, cycle 773 runs, net loss per cycle 5227. 5 cycles = 3865 runs, loss 26135; the 6th contact runs at `net` 36 and needs 36 x j >= 28001 - 26135 = 1866, j = 52; 3865 + 52 = 3917, level 43000 - 26135 - 1872 = 14993. k = 3: contact 304 runs (loss 8696), cycle 904, net 8096 per cycle; 3 cycles = 2712 runs, loss 24288; 36 x j >= 3713, j = 104; 2816, level 43000 - 24288 - 3744 = 14968. k = 4: contact 343 runs (loss 11657), cycle 943, net 11057; 2 cycles = 1886 runs, loss 22114; 36 x j >= 5887, j = 164; 2050, level 43000 - 22114 - 5904 = 14982. No 'about' and no 'at least': each count is asserted exactly in runs (never wall-clock; 9053 runs = 1810.6 s). The 'end of the Nth encounter' figures (13 x 150 + 12 x 600 = 9150 runs for k = 1) are not the first crossing and appear only in D.10 under that label. ### A6. Perception, determinism, audio, closed list (SN-23 to SN-28)

- **AC-SN-73 (auto)**: GIVEN a Sanity double that raises a lie and a false "hull failing" state while `phase` is CALM (phantom, phantom emitter and phantom loot via stubs for the deferred classes) WHEN a run executes THEN `attention.view`, `TellAudit` and `emitters()` are bit-identical to a run without the double. (AC-AT-73, SN-7, SN-22, EC-SN-47)
- **AC-SN-74 (lint)**: GIVEN the codebase WHEN scanned THEN no symbol of `sanity.ledger` or `ledger_listed` is read outside `sanity/` except the Debug Overlay as DIAGNOSTIC, and no symbol of `sanity.perception` is read except by the Filter or the replication adapter *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: `ledger_listed` added)*. (SN-1, SN-23, H5, CR-12)
- **AC-SN-75 (auto)**: GIVEN `post_restore` WHEN it runs THEN `sanity.perception` equals the pre-save projection field for field before the first RUNNING frame (AC-SL-93); GIVEN a ledger whose ACTIVE lie has a well-formed `subject_ref` that no longer resolves THEN the restore **succeeds**, the ledger is unchanged, and `sanity.perception` equals the pre-save projection field for field; GIVEN a malformed `subject_ref` (wrong shape or out-of-range id) THEN the restore fails with `SANITY_RESTORE_SHAPE` and nothing is clamped; `SANITY_RESTORE_SUBJECT_UNRESOLVED` no longer exists in the closed set. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review, BLOCKING; see AC-SN-159 for the integration case.)* (SN-23, EC-SN-30, EC-SN-34)
- **AC-SN-76 (auto, G4)**: GIVEN a 20-minute scripted input session with saves at run N, between hook runs, and while a lie is PENDING, each followed by a restore and a replay of the remaining inputs WHEN `state_hash(PERSIST)` over `sanity.state` and `sanity.ledger` is compared with the uninterrupted run at every checked tick THEN they are equal, **including `last_onset_run` and `last_cid`**; and separately the `sanity.perception` projection (which `state_hash` excludes by design) is equal field by field at every checked tick. Two further cases: (i) the ledger is round-tripped through JSON (integers arrive as floats) and restored, which must succeed and give an equal `state_hash` (SN-1 restore validation: an integer-valued finite float is re-cast to int, a fractional value, NaN or infinity fails `SHAPE`); (ii) the same ledger content built in two different dictionary insertion orders has an equal `state_hash`. (SN-24, EC-SN-28, EC-SN-29) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING and RECOMMENDED)*
- **AC-SN-173 (auto, integration)**: GIVEN a seat at stage 2 (`LIE_GAP_S[2]` 25 s = 125 runs) with a class (b) onset accepted at run R (so `last_onset_run` = R, set on the accept run, not at PENDING to ACTIVE) and a different lit subject whose event would otherwise pass every other gate, and a snapshot taken at R+50 and restored into a fresh kernel, plus an uninterrupted kernel as control WHEN a gated event on that subject is formed at R+124 and, separately, at R+125 THEN in both kernels the R+124 event is dropped by the gap gate with no draw and no counter advance and the R+125 event is processed (inclusive boundary); and GIVEN a seat that moves from compartment X to Y at run R+40, a snapshot at R+41 and a restore THEN the restored kernel does not open a second visit for Y at its next run (`last_cid` = Y) and the uninterrupted kernel agrees on `evented[]`, `entry_pending` and `batch_end_run` at every checked tick; and GIVEN the seat is unbound from R+10 to R+200 THEN `stage_dwell` and `harm_below_runs` are unchanged across the unbound stretch while `last_onset_run` is unchanged and the gap reads as elapsed on rebind. (SN-1, SN-3, SN-16, D.5, EC-SN-28) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING)*
- **AC-SN-174 (auto)**: GIVEN a seat with three live lies (one PENDING, two ACTIVE) in compartment X and one ACTIVE lie in compartment Y, `LIES_MAX` 4, and slots assigned 0, 1, 2 and 3 WHEN the seat stands in X, then walks to Y, then returns to X, and the lie in slot 1 ends while the seat is in Y and a new lie is accepted in X THEN in X `sanity.perception` lists both ACTIVE X entries with `away` false and the Y entry with `away` true (the PENDING one is absent), in ascending `slot` order; after the walk to Y the `away` flags swap and no entry leaves or enters; on the return they swap back, the slot-1 entry leaves only on the run it ends (wherever the seat stands), and the new X accept enters on its activation; each `lie_id` is unique, >= 1, stable and never reused (the new accept takes `next_lie_id`, and the freed slot 1 is the lowest free slot); `reach_lie_active` is true exactly when `stage >= 2` and a listed entry has `away` false, `reach_tier` 2 and is ACTIVE (false in Y if the only `reach_tier` 2 entry is in X, false while the only one is PENDING); and a lie in a compartment the seat is not in changes no field of the projection except its own `away` flag and never changes `reach_lie_active`. (SN-23, SN-14, OQ-SN-21) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G6 GAP, supersedes the 2026-10-27 delegated G1 listing rule)*
- **AC-SN-180 (auto)** *(added 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, Debug Overlay blocker 1)*: GIVEN an ACTIVE lie `lie_id` 7 in compartment A and the seat in A WHEN a hook run completes THEN `ledger_listed(P, 7)` is true and `sanity.perception` holds `lie_id` 7 with `away` false; WHEN the seat moves to B THEN at the next hook run `ledger_listed(P, 7)` is still true, the entry has `away` true, and the ledger row is still ACTIVE with unchanged `start_run` and `end_run` (the accessor and `sanity.perception` membership always agree); WHEN the seat returns THEN it is true with `away` false; a PENDING row and an ended row (EXPIRED, DEBUNKED, INVALIDATED) read false, an unknown id reads false, and once `end_run` passes (away or not) it never reads true again; after save and restore the accessor equals `sanity.perception` membership on the first RUNNING frame; a SHAPE-key scan and the state hash are identical with and without the accessor. (SN-23, OQ-SN-17)

a comfort lie's ledger entry and a Suit cue are never both live beyond that window) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, and the test also records the **maximum seconds the displayed needle stays on the comfort side of the truth after the INVALIDATE** (the glide-return at `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE`, 32 to 38 s expected; - **AC-SN-165 (auto telemetry; ADVISORY on a playtest of at least 30 lies)**: GIVEN the Debug Overlay's lie-end counters (SN-28: ended by L-G or by the witness ending, DEBUNKED by a scan, EXPIRED while in view, EXPIRED unseen; **"in view" is a SIM compartment proxy: the seat's current compartment `cid(P)` equals the subject's compartment on the run the lie expires, and "unseen" is the complement**, since the sim does not know what the camera shows) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED)* WHEN a playtest has produced at least 30 lies THEN the rung mix is reported, with ADVISORY targets: no single end type above 70 percent, scanned at least 20 percent, expired unseen at most 30 percent; a miss re-opens OQ-SN-39 (the scanner's role and its second currency). (SN-18, D.12) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-SN-166 (auto, deferred: Perception Filter)**: GIVEN the glide departure, the glide return, the fringe breath and the lying-digit tick WHEN each is measured in wall-clock seconds at render rates of 30, 60 and 144 Hz with the sim fixed at 60 THEN each rate is within 5 percent (and one frame) of the 60 Hz value and the PR-13 flash clamp holds at every rate; presentation rates must not assume 60 Hz. (SNV-27, SNV-29) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-SN-167 (auto, deferred: Perception Filter)**: GIVEN a scripted walk through a dark and a lit compartment WHEN `lit(ref)` (the sim) and the renderer's drawn-lit set are compared per subject per frame THEN they agree except for a lag of at most `LIT_RECHECK_TICKS` 6 ticks, and each disagreement increments a debug counter `lit_render_mismatch`; Sanity's candidate set reads the sim value, so a gauge the player sees lit but the sim calls unlit (or the reverse) is a defect. (SN-16) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-SN-168 (manual, ADVISORY with sign-off)**: GIVEN a built session with at least 5 testers WHEN a lie ends in the tester's presence and the tester is asked "what told you" and "was that fair" THEN at least 4 of 5 attribute the truth to a physical cause (the hiss, the draught, the scanner reading) and not to "a bug" or "the interface", and at least 4 of 5 describe a lie they trusted as "I should have looked at the edge" rather than "it cheated" (the design-test corollary); a miss re-opens the Player Fantasy design test, not a verdict cue. (Player Fantasy design test sentence) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-SN-169 (auto)**: GIVEN a live COMFORT lie on a DEGRADED subject WHEN the subject is repaired to healthy, and GIVEN a live ALARM lie on a healthy subject WHEN the subject genuinely degrades into the displayed band THEN each is INVALIDATED silently at the next run with no immunity, and no lie is ever re-labelled with the opposite direction. (EC-SN-60, SN-15 L-D) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-SN-170 (auto)**: GIVEN the live defaults WHEN bound (v) is evaluated THEN the lead from the lowest floor 43000 to `SAN_HARM_ENTER` is 778 runs plus the 150-run dwell = 928 runs = 185.6 s against `SAN_FLOOR_TO_HARM_MIN_S` 180 (margin 5.6 s); the ring-2 and ring-1 reading figures are 235.6 s and 241.2 s *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; and a lowest floor of 41964 refuses with `SANITY_BOOT_LEAD_INVARIANT`. (D.4 (v), SN-10) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-SN-171 (auto)**: GIVEN a settled home body at tier 0 and phase CALM with the level at 53000 (the ring-1 floor, stage 2), in ring 1 / 2 / 3 WHEN runs execute THEN the stage-2 exit (level strictly above 57000) is crossed at run 334 / 501 / 1001, the stage-1 exit (strictly above 72000) at run 1584 / 2376 / 4751 and `SAN_MAX` is reached at run 3917 / 5875 / 11750; GIVEN a calm aboard seat from `SAN_MAX` in ring 1 / 2 / 3 THEN stage 2 is entered at run 2369 / 1957 / 1667, and the ring-1 entry run is at most 2400 (the 480 s fuel-floor guarantee, a lower bound on the visit); and the **reachability relation** holds: `SAN_MAX - L_ref_runs x net < STAGE_ENTER[2]` for every ring with `L_ref_runs` read from the registry (Web Map's typical visit length, OQ-SN-44; until registered the test reads the 14 minute low end of D.10, 4200 runs, and reports the read as provisional) and fails with the named reason `STAGE2_UNREACHABLE` if an `ABOARD_EXPOSURE` or `STAGE_ENTER[2]` retune breaks it. - **AC-SN-175 (auto, integration)**: GIVEN the real MVP slice and N = 8 named world seeds (the seed list is a data file `tests/fixtures/sanity/reachability_seeds.json`, fixed for the test) and a scripted calm seat that spends a 14 minute visit aboard each derelict at tier 0 with no breach epochs, walking every lit compartment WHEN the run is replayed THEN, per seed, the count of class (b) onsets by direction on non-lethal DEGRADED subjects is written to the report as COMFORT-count and ALARM-count, the aggregate COMFORT-count over the 8 seeds is at least 1 and the aggregate ALARM-count at least 1 (so a comfort lie is reachable in visit 1 without forcing the stage), and no seed's first lie is COMFORT; the test fails if the aggregate COMFORT-count is 0, which would mean the Pillar 4 anchor is unreachable on a real visit. - **AC-SN-178 (auto, integration)**: GIVEN a `template_release` export built with the project's export preset WHEN the exported build boots headless to the first RUNNING tick THEN every file under `assets/data/sanity/` that the boot assertions read is present in the PCK (the preset `include_filter` lists them), the boot assertions pass with the same verdicts as the editor run, and removing one file from the filter makes the exported build refuse boot with the named `SANITY_BOOT_*` reason and not crash. (Tuning Knobs, D.4, D.8) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED)*

### C. Counts and coverage

MVP-live: 168 criteria (auto 136, lint 18, review 8, manual 6); deferred: 27 (auto 18, review 3, manual 6); total 195. Counted mechanically from the tag of each criterion (first tag word; a tag containing `deferred` counts as deferred wherever it sits, a B-section tag marked `live` counts as live, and the retired row is not counted) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G1/G4/G6: AC-SN-181 (auto) and AC-SN-182 (auto, integration) added live; clauses edited inside AC-SN-25, 123, 150 and 151 change no count; the earlier count was 166 live, 27 deferred, 193 total)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: AC-SN-180 added live auto; clauses edited inside AC-SN-3, 11, 45, 50, 59, 64, 74, 97, 133, 136, 150, 158, 162 and 174 change no count; the earlier count was 165 live, 27 deferred, 192 total)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: AC-SN-36b, 173, 174, 175, 178 added live auto, AC-SN-176 and 179 added live lint, AC-SN-177 added deferred auto; AC-SN-70, 90 and 91 re-tagged auto to review (live), AC-SN-102 re-tagged auto to review (deferred) and AC-SN-106 re-tagged manual to auto (deferred); the earlier count was 158 live, 26 deferred, 184 total, and the two retired ids AC-SN-43 and AC-SN-112 are not counted)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings: AC-SN-171 added live and AC-SN-172 added deferred; the earlier count was 157 live, 25 deferred, 182 total)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: AC-SN-111 and AC-SN-114 moved from deferred to live, AC-SN-112 retired, AC-SN-158 to 165, 169 and 170 added live, AC-SN-166 and 167 added deferred, AC-SN-168 added as a live manual ADVISORY row; the earlier count was 144 live, 26 deferred, 170 total)*. Coverage: every Core Rule SN-1 to SN-30 and every formula D.1 to D.13 has at least one criterion (SN-5 and SN-6 via AC-SN-16, 23, 24, 26 to 31; SN-20 to SN-22 via AC-SN-99 to 105 and 142; SN-26 via AC-SN-117, 138; SN-27 via AC-SN-80, 82, 131, 132; SN-30 via AC-SN-23, 25; D.7 via AC-SN-69, 70, 134; D.13 via AC-SN-106), and every edge case EC-SN-1 to EC-SN-64 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* (EC-SN-60 via AC-SN-169, 61 via AC-SN-163, 62 via AC-SN-48, 63 via AC-SN-164, 64 via AC-SN-181 *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling G4)*) is cited by at least one criterion or by the deferred criteria for its class. `(spike)`: none needed; the physical-world half of AC-SN-71 needs the Graph's fault timings and is a manual playtest (AC-SN-148) until the Graph prototype exists.

### Gaps / untestable as written

- **Perf threshold (QA-14).** AC-SN-97 now asserts a provisional ceiling derived from the registered `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 (median at most 0.5 ms, maximum at most 2 ms) on a `template_release` export, with the first measured baseline kept as a ratchet; the reference machines are RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK (delegated ruling R12, closes X14) and the ceiling is pending spike measurement on them; the technical-director confirms the 0.5 ms median and the percentile from that measurement (OQ-SN-19). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*
- **World-cost thresholds (QA-15).** AC-SN-71 and AC-SN-134 use 1000 hp and 15 s as placeholders; "non-trivial" and "costly but possible" have no designer-confirmed number (OQ-SN-18). The 15 s is bounded from below by the D.7 two-hop worst case (13.0 s) plus a 2 s margin and from above by `WITNESS_HOPS_MAX` 3 (17.5 s), so it is the derived consequence of two hops; the 1000 hp is **not derived** (the Graph's fault timelines give 192 bp per two minutes for a coolant leak and 4800 bp for a bus overload, so a single hp figure cannot hold for all eight faults) and the numeric clause of AC-SN-71 is therefore ADVISORY until each fault gets its own derived figure from its D.15 timeline. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED)*
- **D.13 (QA-6).** The 1500 AU bound fails for any phantom response above 8.33 s, which is 92 percent of the `PHANTOM_LIFE_S` 8 to 20 range; AC-SN-106 now evaluates the formula over the registry constants at 360, 500, 501 and 1200 ticks (the 8.33 s boundary and the 20 s life), and the 20 s case is OQ-SN-20. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, RECOMMENDED)*
- **AC-SN-71 manual half and AC-SN-88, AC-SN-148, AC-SN-165 and AC-SN-168** are playtest observations and cannot be automated; they are ADVISORY with sign-off. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-SN-64** asserts SIM data only; the Filter's non-reaction to the `away` flip is AC-PF-66b. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, BLOCKING)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G6 GAP, supersedes the 2026-10-27 delegated G1 listing rule)*
- **AC-SN-43 (retired).** The stale-token scan of the GDD text is a `/design-review` checklist item, not a game criterion: it is context-sensitive, so no mechanical oracle exists (QA-17). Its literal-scan half survives as AC-SN-42 and AC-SN-96.
- **GDScript behaviour is [UNV].** The engine reference has no scripting module file; integer overflow, signed-shift behaviour and Dictionary iteration order are unverified, which is why AC-SN-2 and AC-SN-76 assert sorted output and counters below 2^53.
- **Filter-dependent criteria.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: status refresh. The Perception Filter GDD exists, `perception-filter.md`, Designed; what is missing is a built Filter.)* No MVP-live criterion can render a lie until the Filter is built; AC-SN-140, 141, 149, 153, 154, 166 and 167 wait for it, and AC-SN-67 and AC-SN-83 run against whatever stand-in the vertical slice ships.

## Open Questions

Owner is `user` (a decision only the user, or the creative-director acting for the user, can take), `agent` (a specialist or sibling GDD) or `GDD` (this document, to be closed by its own rules). Target is `playtest`, `next GDD` or `ADR`. User-decision items come first. Ids are stable; closed ids are listed after the table.

The MVP reachable stage envelope (SN-29 table) is recomputed: a calm ring-1 sortie is stage 0 for 315.8 s, stage 1 until 473.8 s and stage 2 after; ring 2 reaches stage 2 at 391.4 s and ring 3 at 333.4 s; stage 3 needs 3 to 5 breach epochs in rings 1 and 2 and 2 to 3 in ring 3.
